// 运行时配置缓存。所有取值都在 reload() 时从 settings 表读入。
class AppConfig {
public:
    static AppConfig& instance();

    void reload();

    QString mouseTrigger() const;   // right / middle / x1 / x2 / none
    QString keyboardTrigger() const; // 例如 Ctrl+Alt+Space，空表示不启用
    int dragThreshold() const;      // 像素
    int longPressMs() const;        // 长按放弃拦截的阈值
    int wheelHideMs() const;        // 轮盘自动隐藏时间
    int scriptTimeoutMs() const;    // 外部脚本超时

    QString value(const QString& key, const QString& defaultValue = QString()) const;

    // 最小 / 最大值为可选范围约束：只传 (key, defaultValue) 时不做范围限制
    int intValue(const QString& key, int defaultValue,
                 int minimum = -2147483647, int maximum = 2147483647) const;

    QString actionPageHotkey() const; // 动作页呼出快捷键
    QString settingsHotkey() const;   // 设置界面呼出快捷键
    QString forceQuitHotkey() const;  // 强制退出快捷键


private:
    AppConfig() = default;
    QMap<QString, QString> m_values;
};

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/ENGINE/ActionEngine.cpp  (456 行)
// =============================================================================

#include "ActionEngine.h"

#include "ActionRepository.h"
#include "AppConfig.h"
#include "ScriptRunner.h"
#include "VariableResolver.h"
#include "Win32Utils.h"

#include <QApplication>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QJsonArray>
#include <QJsonDocument>
#include <QJsonObject>
#include <QJsonParseError>
#include <QProcess>
#include <QStandardPaths>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QDebug>

#include <shellapi.h>

#include <algorithm>

namespace {

bool needsSelection(const QString& payload) {
    return payload.contains(QLatin1String("{selected}"), Qt::CaseInsensitive) ||
           payload.contains(QLatin1String("{selection}"), Qt::CaseInsensitive);
}

QString findExecutable(const QString& name) {
    return QStandardPaths::findExecutable(name);
}

QString titleCase(const QString& text) {
    QString result;
    result.reserve(text.size());
    bool newWord = true;
    for (QChar ch : text) {
        if (ch.isLetterOrNumber()) {
            result += newWord ? ch.toUpper() : ch;
            newWord = false;
        } else {
            result += ch;
            newWord = true;
        }
    }
    return result;
}

QString reverseText(const QString& text) {
    QVector<uint> codePoints = text.toUcs4();
    std::reverse(codePoints.begin(), codePoints.end());
    return QString::fromUcs4(codePoints.constData(), codePoints.size());
}

int typeFromName(const QString& name) {
    const QString normalized = name.trimmed().toLower();
    if (normalized == QLatin1String("key") || normalized == QLatin1String("keys") ||
        normalized == QLatin1String("hotkey")) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (normalized == QLatin1String("launch") || normalized == QLatin1String("run") ||
        normalized == QLatin1String("program")) {
        return 1;
    }
    if (normalized == QLatin1String("clipboard") || normalized == QLatin1String("transform")) {
        return 2;
    }
    if (normalized == QLatin1String("script")) return 3;
    if (normalized == QLatin1String("pipeline")) return 4;
    if (normalized == QLatin1String("text") || normalized == QLatin1String("type")) return 5;
    return -1;
}

} // namespace

class ActionEngine::PipelineRunner final : public QObject {
public:
    PipelineRunner(ActionEngine* engine, const QJsonArray& steps, const QString& scene, Done done)
        : QObject(engine), m_engine(engine), m_steps(steps), m_scene(scene), m_done(std::move(done)) {}

    void start() { next(); }

private:
    void next() {
        if (!m_done) return;

        if (m_index >= m_steps.size()) {
            finish(true, QStringLiteral("流水线执行完成"));
            return;
        }

        const QJsonValue value = m_steps.at(m_index++);
        if (!value.isObject()) {
            finish(false, QStringLiteral("流水线步骤必须是 JSON 对象"));
            return;
        }
        const QJsonObject step = value.toObject();

        const QJsonValue typeValue = step.value(QStringLiteral("type"));
        if (typeValue.isUndefined()) {
            int delay = step.value(QStringLiteral("wait")).toInt(-1);
            if (delay < 0) delay = step.value(QStringLiteral("delay")).toInt(-1);
            if (delay < 0) {
                finish(false, QStringLiteral("流水线步骤缺少 type 或 wait"));
                return;
            }
            QTimer::singleShot(delay, this, [this] { next(); });
            return;
        }

        Action action;
        action.slot = -1;
        action.name = step.value(QStringLiteral("name")).toString();
        action.payload = step.value(QStringLiteral("payload")).toString();
        action.type = typeValue.isString() ? typeFromName(typeValue.toString())
                                           : typeValue.toInt(-1);

        if (action.type < 0 || action.type > 5) {
            finish(false, QStringLiteral("流水线步骤类型无效: %1").arg(typeValue.toVariant().toString()));
            return;
        }

        m_engine->runAction(action, m_scene, [this](bool ok, const QString& message) {
            if (!ok) {
                finish(false, message);
            } else {
                next();
            }
        });
    }

    void finish(bool ok, const QString& message) {
        if (!m_done) return;
        Done done = std::move(m_done);
        m_done = nullptr;
        done(ok, message);
        deleteLater();
    }

    ActionEngine* m_engine = nullptr;
    QJsonArray m_steps;
    QString m_scene;
    Done m_done;
    int m_index = 0;
};

ActionEngine::ActionEngine(QObject* parent) : QObject(parent) {}

ActionEngine* ActionEngine::instance() {
    static ActionEngine* engine = nullptr;
    if (!engine) {
        engine = new ActionEngine(qApp);
    }
    return engine;
}

void ActionEngine::execute(int slot, const QString& scene) {
    const Action action = ActionRepository::getActionBySlot(slot, scene);
    if (action.type < 0) {
        // 空槽位被划过是正常操作，不弹通知。
        qDebug().noquote() << "槽位" << slot << "没有配置动作";
        return;
    }
    executeAction(action, scene);
}

void ActionEngine::executeAction(const Action& action, const QString& scene) {
    ActionEngine* engine = instance();
    engine->runAction(action, scene, [engine](bool ok, const QString& message) {
        engine->notify(ok, message);
    });
}

void ActionEngine::notify(bool ok, const QString& message) {
    if (ok) {
        qDebug().noquote() << "动作完成:" << message;
    } else {
        qWarning().noquote() << "动作失败:" << message;
    }
    emit actionFinished(ok, message);
}

QString ActionEngine::expand(const QString& text, const QString& scene, const QString& selection) {
    if (text.isEmpty()) return text;

    VariableContext context;
    context.scene = scene;
    context.selection = selection;
    if (text.contains(QLatin1String("{clipboard}"), Qt::CaseInsensitive)) {
        context.clipboard = Win32Utils::getClipboardText();
    }
    return VariableResolver::expand(text, context);
}

void ActionEngine::runAction(const Action& action, const QString& scene, Done done) {
    if (!done) done = [](bool, const QString&) {};

    // 使用次数打点：只有引用动作库的动作带 id。
    // 流水线子步骤、旧表兜底拿到的都是 -1，不会误记。
    if (action.id > 0) {
        ActionRepository::incrementActionUsage(action.id);
    }

    switch (action.type) {
    case 0:
        runKeySequenceAction(action.payload, scene, std::move(done));
        break;
    case 1:
        runLaunchAction(action.payload, scene, std::move(done));
        break;
    case 2:
        runClipboardAction(action.payload, scene, std::move(done));
        break;
    case 3:
        runScriptAction(action.payload, scene, std::move(done));
        break;
    case 4:
        runPipelineAction(action.payload, scene, std::move(done));
        break;
    case 5:
        runTextAction(action.payload, scene, std::move(done));
        break;
    default:
        done(false, QStringLiteral("未知动作类型: %1").arg(action.type));
        break;
    }
}

void ActionEngine::runKeySequenceAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done) {
    const QString selection = needsSelection(payload) ? Win32Utils::captureSelectedText() : QString();
    const QString sequence = expand(payload, scene, selection).trimmed();

    if (sequence.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("按键序列为空"));
        return;
    }

    QString error;
    if (!Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(sequence, &error)) {
        done(false, error);
        return;
    }
    done(true, QStringLiteral("已发送按键 %1").arg(sequence));
}

void ActionEngine::runLaunchAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done) {
    const QString selection = needsSelection(payload) ? Win32Utils::captureSelectedText() : QString();
    const QString command = expand(payload, scene, selection).trimmed();

    if (command.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("启动命令为空"));
        return;
    }

    if (command.compare(QLatin1String("exit"), Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0) {
        QTimer::singleShot(0, qApp, &QCoreApplication::quit);
        done(true, QStringLiteral("正在退出程序"));
        return;
    }

    const QString lower = command.toLower();
    const bool isUrl = lower.startsWith(QLatin1String("http://")) ||
                       lower.startsWith(QLatin1String("https://")) ||
                       lower.startsWith(QLatin1String("mailto:")) ||
                       lower.startsWith(QLatin1String("shell:"));

    auto shellOpen = [&command](const QString& file) {
        const HINSTANCE result = ShellExecuteW(nullptr, L"open",
                                               reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(file.utf16()),
                                               nullptr, nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
        return reinterpret_cast<INT_PTR>(result) > 32;
    };

    if (isUrl) {
        if (!shellOpen(command)) {
            done(false, QStringLiteral("无法打开链接: %1").arg(command));
            return;
        }
        done(true, QStringLiteral("已打开 %1").arg(command));
        return;
    }

    const QStringList parts = QProcess::splitCommand(command);
    if (parts.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("启动命令为空"));
        return;
    }

    QString program = parts.first();
    QStringList arguments = parts.mid(1);

    if (!QFileInfo(program).isAbsolute() && !program.contains(QLatin1Char('/')) &&
        !program.contains(QLatin1Char('\\'))) {
        const QString found = findExecutable(program);
        if (!found.isEmpty()) program = found;
    }

    const bool hasArguments = !arguments.isEmpty();
    const bool exists = QFileInfo::exists(program);

    bool ok = false;
    if (hasArguments) {
        ok = QProcess::startDetached(program, arguments);
    } else if (exists) {
        ok = shellOpen(program);
    } else {
        ok = QProcess::startDetached(program, {});
    }

    if (!ok) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("无法启动: %1").arg(command));
        return;
    }
    done(true, QStringLiteral("已启动 %1").arg(command));
}

void ActionEngine::runClipboardAction(const QString& payload, const QString&, Done done) {
    const QString operation = payload.trimmed().toLower();
    if (operation.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("剪贴板转换参数为空"));
        return;
    }

    const QString oldClipboard = Win32Utils::getClipboardText();
    const QString selected = Win32Utils::captureSelectedText();
    if (selected.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("没有选中可转换的文字"));
        return;
    }

    QString result;
    if (operation == QLatin1String("upper")) {
        result = selected.toUpper();
    } else if (operation == QLatin1String("lower")) {
        result = selected.toLower();
    } else if (operation == QLatin1String("title")) {
        result = titleCase(selected);
    } else if (operation == QLatin1String("trim")) {
        result = selected.trimmed();
    } else if (operation == QLatin1String("reverse")) {
        result = reverseText(selected);
    } else {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("未知剪贴板操作“%1”。可用：upper / lower / title / trim / reverse")
                       .arg(operation));
        return;
    }

    if (!Win32Utils::setClipboardText(result)) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("写入剪贴板失败"));
        return;
    }

    Win32Utils::sendKey('V', true);

    // 等目标程序读完剪贴板，再恢复原内容，最后才报告完成。
    // 这样流水线的下一步不会和剪贴板恢复相互抢时序。
    QTimer::singleShot(250, this,
                       [oldClipboard, result, done = std::move(done)]() mutable {
                           if (Win32Utils::getClipboardText() == result) {
                               Win32Utils::setClipboardText(oldClipboard);
                           }
                           done(true, QStringLiteral("剪贴板转换完成（%1 个字符）").arg(result.size()));
                       });
}

void ActionEngine::runTextAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done) {
    const QString selection = needsSelection(payload) ? Win32Utils::captureSelectedText() : QString();
    const QString text = expand(payload, scene, selection);

    if (text.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("要输入的文本为空"));
        return;
    }

    QString error;
    if (!Win32Utils::sendText(text, &error)) {
        done(false, error);
        return;
    }
    done(true, QStringLiteral("已输入 %1 个字符").arg(text.size()));
}

void ActionEngine::runScriptAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done) {
    // 先保存原始剪贴板，再抓取选中文字（captureSelectedText 会恢复剪贴板）。
    const QString oldClipboard = Win32Utils::getClipboardText();
    const QString selection = Win32Utils::captureSelectedText();
    const QString command = expand(payload, scene, selection).trimmed();

    if (command.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("脚本命令为空"));
        return;
    }

    auto* runner = new ScriptRunner(this);
    runner->run(command, selection, AppConfig::instance().scriptTimeoutMs(),
                [this, runner, oldClipboard, done = std::move(done)](
                    const ScriptRunner::Result& result) mutable {
                    runner->deleteLater();

                    if (!result.ok) {
                        done(false, result.error);
                        return;
                    }

                    const QString output = result.stdoutText.trimmed();
                    if (output.isEmpty()) {
                        done(true, QStringLiteral("脚本执行完成（没有输出，未粘贴）"));
                        return;
                    }

                    if (!Win32Utils::setClipboardText(output)) {
                        done(false, QStringLiteral("脚本已执行，但写入剪贴板失败"));
                        return;
                    }

                    Win32Utils::sendKey('V', true);
                    QTimer::singleShot(
                        250, this,
                        [oldClipboard, output, done = std::move(done)]() mutable {
                            if (Win32Utils::getClipboardText() == output) {
                                Win32Utils::setClipboardText(oldClipboard);
                            }
                            done(true, QStringLiteral("脚本输出已粘贴（%1 个字符）")
                                           .arg(output.size()));
                        });
                });
}

void ActionEngine::runPipelineAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done) {
    QJsonParseError parseError;
    const QJsonDocument document = QJsonDocument::fromJson(payload.toUtf8(), &parseError);

    if (parseError.error != QJsonParseError::NoError) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("流水线 JSON 解析失败: %1（位置 %2）")
                       .arg(parseError.errorString())
                       .arg(parseError.offset));
        return;
    }

    QJsonArray steps;
    if (document.isArray()) {
        steps = document.array();
    } else if (document.isObject()) {
        steps = document.object().value(QStringLiteral("steps")).toArray();
    }

    if (steps.isEmpty()) {
        done(false, QStringLiteral("流水线没有任何步骤"));
        return;
    }

    auto* runner = new PipelineRunner(this, steps, scene, std::move(done));
    runner->start();
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/ENGINE/ActionEngine.h  (46 行)
// =============================================================================

#pragma once

#include <QObject>
#include <QString>

#include <functional>

#include "ActionModel.h"

// 动作执行引擎。所有耗时操作（外部脚本）都是异步的，不阻塞 UI。
class ActionEngine : public QObject {
    Q_OBJECT

public:
    using Done = std::function<void(bool ok, const QString& message)>;

    explicit ActionEngine(QObject* parent = nullptr);

    static ActionEngine* instance();

    // 轮盘选中某个槽位后调用。
    static void execute(int slot, const QString& scene);

    // 直接执行一个动作（鼠标高级绑定也走这里）。
    static void executeAction(const Action& action, const QString& scene);

signals:
    // 顶层动作执行结束。失败时 UI 可以弹托盘提示。
    void actionFinished(bool ok, const QString& message);

private:
    class PipelineRunner;

    void runAction(const Action& action, const QString& scene, Done done);

    void runKeySequenceAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done);
    void runLaunchAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done);
    void runClipboardAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done);
    void runScriptAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done);
    void runTextAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done);
    void runPipelineAction(const QString& payload, const QString& scene, Done done);

    void notify(bool ok, const QString& message);

    static QString expand(const QString& text, const QString& scene, const QString& selection);
};

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/ENGINE/ScriptRunner.cpp  (257 行)
// =============================================================================

#include "ScriptRunner.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QProcess>
#include <QProcessEnvironment>
#include <QStandardPaths>
#include <QTimer>

#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#include <windows.h>

namespace {

QString findExecutable(const QString& name) {
    return QStandardPaths::findExecutable(name);
}

void killProcessTree(QProcess* process) {
    if (!process) return;

    const qint64 pid = process->processId();
    if (pid <= 0) {
        process->kill();
        return;
    }

    // 用 startDetached 异步结束整棵进程树，并隐藏 taskkill 的控制台窗口。
    QProcess killer;
    killer.setProgram(QStringLiteral("taskkill.exe"));
    killer.setArguments({QStringLiteral("/PID"), QString::number(pid),
                         QStringLiteral("/T"), QStringLiteral("/F")});
    killer.setStandardOutputFile(QProcess::nullDevice());
    killer.setStandardErrorFile(QProcess::nullDevice());
    killer.setCreateProcessArgumentsModifier([](QProcess::CreateProcessArguments* arguments) {
        arguments->flags |= CREATE_NO_WINDOW;
    });
    killer.startDetached();
}

} // namespace

ScriptCommand ScriptCommand::resolve(const QString& commandLine) {
    ScriptCommand command;

    const QStringList parts = QProcess::splitCommand(commandLine);
    if (parts.isEmpty()) {
        command.error = QStringLiteral("命令为空");
        return command;
    }

    QStringList arguments = parts;
    const QString first = arguments.takeFirst();
    const QFileInfo fileInfo(first);
    const QString extension = fileInfo.suffix().toLower();

    command.workingDir = fileInfo.exists() ? fileInfo.absolutePath() : QDir::currentPath();

    auto useInterpreter = [&](const QString& executable, const QStringList& options) {
        command.program = executable;
        command.arguments = options + QStringList{first} + arguments;
    };

    if (extension == QLatin1String("py") || extension == QLatin1String("pyw")) {
        QString python = findExecutable(extension == QLatin1String("pyw")
                                            ? QStringLiteral("pythonw")
                                            : QStringLiteral("python"));
        if (python.isEmpty()) python = findExecutable(QStringLiteral("py"));
        if (python.isEmpty()) {
            command.error = QStringLiteral("找不到 Python。请安装 Python，或把 python.exe 加入 PATH。");
            return command;
        }

        const QStringList options =
            QFileInfo(python).fileName().compare(QLatin1String("py.exe"), Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0
                ? QStringList{QStringLiteral("-3")}
                : QStringList{};
        useInterpreter(python, options);
    } else if (extension == QLatin1String("js") || extension == QLatin1String("mjs") ||
               extension == QLatin1String("cjs")) {
        const QString node = findExecutable(QStringLiteral("node"));
        if (node.isEmpty()) {
            command.error = QStringLiteral("找不到 Node.js。请安装 Node.js，或把 node.exe 加入 PATH。");
            return command;
        }
        useInterpreter(node, {});
    } else if (extension == QLatin1String("ps1")) {
        QString powershell = findExecutable(QStringLiteral("powershell"));
        if (powershell.isEmpty()) powershell = findExecutable(QStringLiteral("pwsh"));
        if (powershell.isEmpty()) {
            command.error = QStringLiteral("找不到 PowerShell。");
            return command;
        }
        useInterpreter(powershell,
                       {QStringLiteral("-NoProfile"), QStringLiteral("-ExecutionPolicy"),
                        QStringLiteral("Bypass"), QStringLiteral("-File")});
    } else if (extension == QLatin1String("bat") || extension == QLatin1String("cmd")) {
        const QString cmd = findExecutable(QStringLiteral("cmd"));
        if (cmd.isEmpty()) {
            command.error = QStringLiteral("找不到 cmd.exe。");
            return command;
        }
        useInterpreter(cmd, {QStringLiteral("/d"), QStringLiteral("/c")});
    } else if (extension == QLatin1String("vbs")) {
        const QString cscript = findExecutable(QStringLiteral("cscript"));
        if (cscript.isEmpty()) {
            command.error = QStringLiteral("找不到 cscript.exe。");
            return command;
        }
        useInterpreter(cscript, {QStringLiteral("//nologo")});
    } else {
        // .exe 或 PATH 中的命令（python -u xxx.py / notepad.exe / node ...）。
        command.program = first;
        command.arguments = arguments;
    }

    if (command.program.isEmpty()) {
        command.error = QStringLiteral("无法解析脚本命令");
    }
    return command;
}

ScriptRunner::ScriptRunner(QObject* parent) : QObject(parent) {}

ScriptRunner::~ScriptRunner() {
    if (m_process && m_process->state() != QProcess::NotRunning) {
        m_process->kill();
    }
}

void ScriptRunner::run(const QString& commandLine, const QString& stdinText, int timeoutMs,
                       Callback callback) {
    if (m_process || m_finished) return;

    m_callback = std::move(callback);
    m_timeoutMs = qMax(200, timeoutMs);

    const ScriptCommand command = ScriptCommand::resolve(commandLine);
    if (!command.valid()) {
        m_result.error = command.error.isEmpty() ? QStringLiteral("脚本命令无效") : command.error;
        finish();
        return;
    }

    m_process = new QProcess(this);
    m_process->setProgram(command.program);
    m_process->setArguments(command.arguments);
    if (!command.workingDir.isEmpty()) {
        m_process->setWorkingDirectory(command.workingDir);
    }
    m_process->setProcessChannelMode(QProcess::SeparateChannels);

    // 统一编码，避免中文在 Python / Node 管道里乱码。
    QProcessEnvironment environment = QProcessEnvironment::systemEnvironment();
    environment.insert(QStringLiteral("PYTHONIOENCODING"), QStringLiteral("utf-8"));
    environment.insert(QStringLiteral("PYTHONUTF8"), QStringLiteral("1"));
    environment.insert(QStringLiteral("PYTHONUNBUFFERED"), QStringLiteral("1"));
    m_process->setProcessEnvironment(environment);

    connect(m_process, &QProcess::started, this, [this, stdinText] {
        if (!m_process) return;
        if (!stdinText.isEmpty()) {
            m_process->write(stdinText.toUtf8());
        }
        m_process->closeWriteChannel();
    });

    connect(m_process, &QProcess::errorOccurred, this, [this](QProcess::ProcessError error) {
        if (m_finished || error != QProcess::FailedToStart) return;

        m_result.error = QStringLiteral("脚本启动失败: %1")
                             .arg(m_process ? m_process->errorString() : QStringLiteral("未知错误"));
        finish();
    });

    connect(m_process, &QProcess::finished, this,
            [this](int exitCode, QProcess::ExitStatus exitStatus) {
                if (m_finished) return;

                m_result.exitCode = exitCode;
                if (m_process) {
                    m_result.stdoutText = QString::fromUtf8(m_process->readAllStandardOutput());
                    m_result.stderrText = QString::fromUtf8(m_process->readAllStandardError());
                }

                if (m_timedOut) {
                    m_result.timedOut = true;
                    m_result.error = QStringLiteral("脚本执行超时（%1 ms），已强制结束进程")
                                         .arg(m_timeoutMs);
                } else if (exitStatus == QProcess::CrashExit) {
                    m_result.crashed = true;
                    m_result.error = QStringLiteral("脚本异常退出");
                    if (!m_result.stderrText.trimmed().isEmpty()) {
                        m_result.error += QStringLiteral(": ") + m_result.stderrText.trimmed();
                    }
                } else if (exitCode != 0) {
                    m_result.error = QStringLiteral("脚本退出码 %1").arg(exitCode);
                    if (!m_result.stderrText.trimmed().isEmpty()) {
                        m_result.error += QStringLiteral(": ") + m_result.stderrText.trimmed();
                    }
                } else {
                    m_result.ok = true;
                }

                finish();
            });

    m_timeoutTimer = new QTimer(this);
    m_timeoutTimer->setSingleShot(true);
    connect(m_timeoutTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] {
        if (m_finished || !m_process) return;

        m_timedOut = true;
        killProcessTree(m_process);

        // 极少数情况下 taskkill 也结束不了，再兜底一次。
        QTimer::singleShot(2000, this, [this] {
            if (m_finished) return;
            m_result.error = QStringLiteral("脚本执行超时（%1 ms），且无法结束进程")
                                 .arg(m_timeoutMs);
            finish();
        });
    });
    m_timeoutTimer->start(m_timeoutMs);

    m_process->start();
}

void ScriptRunner::finish() {
    if (m_finished) return;
    m_finished = true;

    if (m_timeoutTimer) {
        m_timeoutTimer->stop();
        m_timeoutTimer->deleteLater();
        m_timeoutTimer = nullptr;
    }

    if (m_process) {
        if (m_process->state() != QProcess::NotRunning) {
            killProcessTree(m_process);
        }
        m_process->deleteLater();
        m_process = nullptr;
    }

    if (m_callback) {
        Callback callback = std::move(m_callback);
        m_callback = nullptr;
        callback(m_result);
    }
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/ENGINE/ScriptRunner.h  (64 行)
// =============================================================================

#pragma once

#include <QObject>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>

#include <functional>

class QProcess;
class QTimer;

// 把用户填写的“脚本参数”解析成可执行的 program + arguments。
// 支持：.py / .pyw / .js / .mjs / .cjs / .ps1 / .bat / .cmd / .vbs / .exe，
// 也支持直接写 "python xxx.py" 这类完整命令行。
struct ScriptCommand {
    QString program;
    QStringList arguments;
    QString workingDir;
    QString error;

    bool valid() const { return error.isEmpty() && !program.isEmpty(); }

    static ScriptCommand resolve(const QString& commandLine);
};

// 异步执行一个脚本：
//   stdin  <- UTF-8 文本
//   stdout -> UTF-8 文本（由调用方决定是否粘贴）
//   超时会杀死整个进程树，不会卡住主线程。
class ScriptRunner : public QObject {
public:
    struct Result {
        bool ok = false;
        bool timedOut = false;
        bool crashed = false;
        int exitCode = -1;
        QString stdoutText;
        QString stderrText;
        QString error;
    };

    using Callback = std::function<void(const Result& result)>;

    explicit ScriptRunner(QObject* parent = nullptr);
    ~ScriptRunner() override;

    void run(const QString& commandLine, const QString& stdinText, int timeoutMs,
             Callback callback);

    static ScriptCommand resolve(const QString& commandLine) {
        return ScriptCommand::resolve(commandLine);
    }

private:
    void finish();

    QProcess* m_process = nullptr;
    QTimer* m_timeoutTimer = nullptr;
    bool m_finished = false;
    bool m_timedOut = false;
    int m_timeoutMs = 8000;
    Callback m_callback;
    Result m_result;
};

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/ENGINE/VariableResolver.cpp  (98 行)
// =============================================================================

#include "VariableResolver.h"

#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QUuid>
#include <QtGlobal>

namespace {

QString braceValue(const QString& key, const VariableContext& context) {
    const QString name = key.trimmed().toLower();

    if (name == QLatin1String("clipboard")) return context.clipboard;
    if (name == QLatin1String("selected") || name == QLatin1String("selection")) {
        return context.selection;
    }
    if (name == QLatin1String("scene") || name == QLatin1String("app") ||
        name == QLatin1String("process")) {
        return context.scene;
    }
    if (name == QLatin1String("date")) return context.now.toString(QStringLiteral("yyyy-MM-dd"));
    if (name == QLatin1String("time")) return context.now.toString(QStringLiteral("HH:mm:ss"));
    if (name == QLatin1String("datetime")) {
        return context.now.toString(QStringLiteral("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"));
    }
    if (name == QLatin1String("timestamp")) {
        return QString::number(context.now.toSecsSinceEpoch());
    }
    if (name == QLatin1String("uuid")) {
        return QUuid::createUuid().toString(QUuid::WithoutBraces);
    }
    if (name == QLatin1String("newline") || name == QLatin1String("nl")) {
        return QStringLiteral("\n");
    }
    if (name == QLatin1String("tab")) return QStringLiteral("\t");
    if (name.startsWith(QLatin1String("env:"))) {
        const QString envName = key.trimmed().mid(4);
        const QByteArray encoded = envName.toUtf8();
        if (qEnvironmentVariableIsSet(encoded.constData())) {
            return qEnvironmentVariable(encoded.constData());
        }
        return QStringLiteral("{%1}").arg(key);
    }

    return QStringLiteral("{%1}").arg(key); // 未知变量原样保留
}

} // namespace

QString VariableResolver::expand(const QString& input, const VariableContext& context) {
    QString result;
    result.reserve(input.size());

    int index = 0;
    while (index < input.size()) {
        const QChar ch = input.at(index);

        if (ch == QLatin1Char('{')) {
            const int close = input.indexOf(QLatin1Char('}'), index + 1);
            if (close > index) {
                const QString key = input.mid(index + 1, close - index - 1);
                // 只把“像变量名”的内容当变量，正文里的 json / 代码块保持原样。
                static const QRegularExpression namePattern(
                    QStringLiteral("^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_:.-]*$"));
                if (namePattern.match(key).hasMatch()) {
                    result += braceValue(key, context);
                    index = close + 1;
                    continue;
                }
            }
            result += ch;
            ++index;
            continue;
        }

        if (ch == QLatin1Char('%')) {
            const int close = input.indexOf(QLatin1Char('%'), index + 1);
            if (close > index + 1) {
                const QString name = input.mid(index + 1, close - index - 1);
                if (!name.contains(QLatin1Char('%'))) {
                    const QByteArray encoded = name.toUtf8();
                    if (qEnvironmentVariableIsSet(encoded.constData())) {
                        result += qEnvironmentVariable(encoded.constData());
                        index = close + 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                }
            }
            result += ch;
            ++index;
            continue;
        }

        result += ch;
        ++index;
    }

    return result;
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/ENGINE/VariableResolver.h  (26 行)
// =============================================================================

#pragma once

#include <QDateTime>
#include <QString>

// 变量替换的上下文。所有字段在展开前由调用方填好。
struct VariableContext {
    QString scene;      // 当前场景，通常是前台进程名，例如 Code.exe
    QString selection;  // 当前选中的文字（可能为空）
    QString clipboard;  // 当前剪贴板文字（可能为空）
    QDateTime now = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
};

// 支持的动作参数变量：
//   {clipboard}            当前剪贴板
//   {selected} / {selection} 当前选中文字
//   {scene} / {app} / {process} 当前前台进程名
//   {date} {time} {datetime} {timestamp} {uuid}
//   {newline} {tab}
//   {env:NAME}             环境变量
//   %NAME%                 Windows 风格环境变量（兼容旧写法）
// 未知变量原样保留，避免误伤用户文本。
class VariableResolver {
public:
    static QString expand(const QString& input, const VariableContext& context);
};

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/HOOK/HookManager.cpp  (74 行)
// =============================================================================

#include "HookManager.h"

HookManager* HookManager::s_instance = nullptr;

HookManager::~HookManager() {
    uninstall();
}

bool HookManager::install(MouseCallback callback) {
    m_callback = std::move(callback);
    s_instance = this;

    m_hHook = SetWindowsHookEx(WH_MOUSE_LL, LowLevelMouseProc, nullptr, 0);
    return m_hHook != nullptr;
}

bool HookManager::installKeyboard(KeyboardCallback callback) {
    m_keyCallback = std::move(callback);
    s_instance = this;

    m_hKeyboard = SetWindowsHookEx(WH_KEYBOARD_LL, LowLevelKeyboardProc, nullptr, 0);
    return m_hKeyboard != nullptr;
}

void HookManager::uninstallMouse() {
    if (m_hHook) {
        UnhookWindowsHookEx(m_hHook);
        m_hHook = nullptr;
    }
    m_callback = nullptr;
}

void HookManager::uninstallKeyboard() {
    if (m_hKeyboard) {
        UnhookWindowsHookEx(m_hKeyboard);
        m_hKeyboard = nullptr;
    }
    m_keyCallback = nullptr;
}

void HookManager::uninstall() {
    uninstallMouse();
    uninstallKeyboard();
    s_instance = nullptr;
}

LRESULT CALLBACK HookManager::LowLevelMouseProc(int nCode, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam) {
    if (nCode == HC_ACTION && s_instance && s_instance->m_callback) {
        auto* mouse = reinterpret_cast<MSLLHOOKSTRUCT*>(lParam);

        // 过滤本程序 SendInput 产生的注入事件，避免自己触发自己（死循环）。
        if (!(mouse->flags & LLMHF_INJECTED)) {
            if (s_instance->m_callback(wParam, mouse)) {
                return 1; // 吞掉事件，阻止原生右键菜单等
            }
        }
    }

    return CallNextHookEx(nullptr, nCode, wParam, lParam);
}

LRESULT CALLBACK HookManager::LowLevelKeyboardProc(int nCode, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam) {
    if (nCode == HC_ACTION && s_instance && s_instance->m_keyCallback) {
        auto* keyboard = reinterpret_cast<KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT*>(lParam);

        if (!(keyboard->flags & (LLKHF_INJECTED | LLKHF_LOWER_IL_INJECTED))) {
            if (s_instance->m_keyCallback(wParam, keyboard)) {
                return 1; // 吞掉热键，避免字符输入到目标窗口
            }
        }
    }

    return CallNextHookEx(nullptr, nCode, wParam, lParam);
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/HOOK/HookManager.h  (45 行)
// =============================================================================

#pragma once

#include <functional>

#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#include <windows.h>

class HookManager {
public:
    using MouseCallback = std::function<bool(WPARAM, MSLLHOOKSTRUCT*)>;
    using KeyboardCallback = std::function<bool(WPARAM, KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT*)>;

    HookManager() = default;
    ~HookManager();

    // 安装低级鼠标钩子（WH_MOUSE_LL）。
    bool install(MouseCallback callback);

    // 安装低级键盘钩子（WH_KEYBOARD_LL），用于全局热键。
    bool installKeyboard(KeyboardCallback callback);

    // 卸载全部钩子。
    void uninstall();

private:
    // 给 Windows 调用的静态函数没有 this，通过 s_instance 找到对象。
    static LRESULT CALLBACK LowLevelMouseProc(int nCode, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam);
    static LRESULT CALLBACK LowLevelKeyboardProc(int nCode, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam);

    void uninstallMouse();
    void uninstallKeyboard();

    HHOOK m_hHook = nullptr;
    HHOOK m_hKeyboard = nullptr;

    MouseCallback m_callback;
    KeyboardCallback m_keyCallback;

    static HookManager* s_instance;
};

// 定义在 MainWindow.cpp（全局作用域），呼出动作页与设置界面共用同一个"新建动作页"弹窗
int showNewPageDialog(QWidget* parent, const QString& currentScene);
#include <QMessageBox>      // 新增

namespace {
constexpr char kActionMime[] = "application/x-huan-action-id";
// 格子间拖拽交换：携带源格子索引
constexpr char kPageIndexMime[] = "application/x-huan-page-index";
constexpr int kCols = 4;
constexpr int kRows = 6;
constexpr int kPageSize = kCols * kRows;
constexpr int kCollapsedSize = 64; // 桌面图标大小

// 给单色图标染色。QPushButton 样式里的 color 只作用于文字，对 QIcon 完全无效，
// 所以"已固定"的蓝色必须直接染到 pixmap 上，改 QSS 是没用的。
QPixmap tintedIcon(const QString& fileName, const QColor& color) {
    const QPixmap src(AppConstants::getUiIconPath(fileName));
    if (src.isNull()) return QPixmap();
    QPixmap out(src.size());
    out.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter p(&out);
    p.drawPixmap(0, 0, src);
    p.setCompositionMode(QPainter::CompositionMode_SourceIn);
    p.fillRect(out.rect(), color);
    p.end();
    return out;
}

// 固定按钮图标：优先用成对素材（pin_pinned.png / pin_unpinned.png）；
// 成对素材缺失时，拿 pin_unpinned.png 的轮廓分别染成蓝 / 灰兜底 —— 单色图也能看出状态。
void applyPinIcon(QPushButton* btn, bool pinned) {
    if (!btn) return;
    const QPixmap custom(AppConstants::getUiIconPath(
        pinned ? QStringLiteral("pin_pinned.png") : QStringLiteral("pin_unpinned.png")));
    if (!custom.isNull()) {
        btn->setIcon(QIcon(custom));
        return;
    }
    const QPixmap tinted = tintedIcon(QStringLiteral("pin_unpinned.png"),
                                      pinned ? QColor(0x19, 0x76, 0xD2)
                                             : QColor(0x5F, 0x63, 0x68));
    if (!tinted.isNull()) btn->setIcon(QIcon(tinted));
}

QString typeName(int type) {
    switch (type) {
    case 0: return QStringLiteral("按键");
    case 1: return QStringLiteral("程序");
    case 2: return QStringLiteral("剪贴板");
    case 3: return QStringLiteral("脚本");
    case 4: return QStringLiteral("流水线");
    case 5: return QStringLiteral("文本");
    default: return QStringLiteral("");
    }
}

// 轻量级动作编辑弹窗
class PageActionEditDialog : public QDialog {
public:
    PageActionEditDialog(QWidget* parent, const LibraryAction& initial, bool isNew)
        : QDialog(parent), m_action(initial) {
        setWindowTitle(isNew ? QStringLiteral("新增动作") : QStringLiteral("编辑动作"));
        resize(400, 200);
        auto* layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
        auto* form = new QFormLayout();
        
        m_name = new QLineEdit(initial.name, this);
        form->addRow(QStringLiteral("名字:"), m_name);

        m_type = new QComboBox(this);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("按键组合"), 0);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("运行程序/打开链接"), 1);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("剪贴板转换"), 2);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("外部脚本"), 3);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("多步流水线"), 4);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("输入文本"), 5);
        form->addRow(QStringLiteral("类型:"), m_type);

        m_payload = new QLineEdit(initial.payload, this);
        form->addRow(QStringLiteral("参数:"), m_payload);

        layout->addLayout(form);
        auto* buttons = new QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox::Ok | QDialogButtonBox::Cancel, this);
        layout->addWidget(buttons);

        connect(buttons, &QDialogButtonBox::accepted, this, [this] { 
            if (m_name->text().trimmed().isEmpty()) {
                QMessageBox::warning(this, "错误", "名字不能为空");
                return;
            }
            accept(); 
        });
        connect(buttons, &QDialogButtonBox::rejected, this, &QDialog::reject);

        int idx = m_type->findData(initial.type >= 0 ? initial.type : 0);
        m_type->setCurrentIndex(idx >= 0 ? idx : 0);
    }

    LibraryAction result() const {
        LibraryAction a = m_action;
        a.name = m_name->text().trimmed();
        a.type = m_type->currentData().toInt();
        a.payload = m_payload->text().trimmed();
        return a;
    }
private:
    LibraryAction m_action;
    QLineEdit* m_name;
    QComboBox* m_type;
    QLineEdit* m_payload;
};

class ActionButton : public QPushButton {
public:
    ActionButton(int index, int actionId = -1, QWidget* parent = nullptr)
        : QPushButton(parent), m_index(index), m_actionId(actionId) {
        setAcceptDrops(true);
    }
    std::function<void(int index, int actionId)> onActionDropped;
    std::function<void(int srcIndex, int dstIndex)> onActionSwapped;

protected:
    void mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent* event) override {
        if (event->button() == Qt::LeftButton) {
            m_dragStartPosition = event->pos();
        }
        QPushButton::mousePressEvent(event);
    }
    void mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent* event) override {
        if (!(event->buttons() & Qt::LeftButton)) return;
        if ((event->pos() - m_dragStartPosition).manhattanLength() < QApplication::startDragDistance()) return;
        if (m_actionId <= 0) return;

        QDrag* drag = new QDrag(this);
        QMimeData* mimeData = new QMimeData;
        mimeData->setData(kActionMime, QByteArray::number(m_actionId));
        mimeData->setData(kPageIndexMime, QByteArray::number(m_index));
        drag->setMimeData(mimeData);
        drag->exec(Qt::CopyAction | Qt::MoveAction);
    }
    void dragEnterEvent(QDragEnterEvent* event) override {
        if (event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kPageIndexMime) ||
            event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kActionMime)) {
            event->acceptProposedAction();
        }
    }
    void dragMoveEvent(QDragMoveEvent* event) override {
        if (event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kPageIndexMime) ||
            event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kActionMime)) {
            event->acceptProposedAction();
        }
    }
    void dropEvent(QDropEvent* event) override {
        // 优先处理"页内格子交换"
        if (event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kPageIndexMime)) {
            const int srcIndex = event->mimeData()->data(kPageIndexMime).toInt();
            if (srcIndex >= 0 && srcIndex != m_index && onActionSwapped) {
                onActionSwapped(srcIndex, m_index);
            }
            event->acceptProposedAction();
            return;
        }
        if (event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kActionMime)) {
            int actionId = event->mimeData()->data(kActionMime).toInt();
            if (actionId > 0 && onActionDropped) onActionDropped(m_index, actionId);
            event->acceptProposedAction();
        }
    }
private:
    int m_index;
    int m_actionId = -1;
    QPoint m_dragStartPosition;
};
} // namespace

ActionPage::ActionPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent) {
    setWindowFlags(Qt::Tool | Qt::WindowStaysOnTopHint | Qt::FramelessWindowHint);
    setAttribute(Qt::WA_TranslucentBackground);

    auto* mainLayout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    mainLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    mainLayout->setSpacing(0);

    // --- 1. 折叠时的图标 ---
    m_logoLabel = new QLabel(this);
    m_logoLabel->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    m_logoLabel->setStyleSheet("background: transparent;");
    
    // 统一走 Constants，折叠态 logo 与托盘/窗口图标同一个文件
    QIcon icon(AppConstants::getMainIconPath());
    if (!icon.isNull()) {
        m_logoLabel->setPixmap(icon.pixmap(kCollapsedSize, kCollapsedSize));
    } else {
        m_logoLabel->setText("MM"); 
        m_logoLabel->setStyleSheet("color: #1976D2; font-weight: bold; font-size: 20px; background: transparent;");
    }
    m_logoLabel->hide();
    mainLayout->addWidget(m_logoLabel);

    // --- 2. 展开时的面板 ---
    m_container = new QWidget(this);
    m_container->setObjectName("ActionPageMain");
    m_container->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral(
        "QWidget#ActionPageMain { background-color: #F8F9FA; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #D0D0D0; }"
        "QLabel { background: transparent; }"
    ));
    
    auto* layout = new QVBoxLayout(m_container);
    layout->setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10);

    // 顶部标题栏
    auto* topBar = new QHBoxLayout();
    m_titleLabel = new QLabel(this);
    m_titleLabel->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral("font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold; color: #333;"));
    
    // 获取图标路径的辅助 Lambda：统一走 Constants，不再自己拼路径
    auto getIcon = [](const QString& name) {
        return QIcon(AppConstants::getUiIconPath(name));
    };
    m_pinBtn = new QPushButton(this);
    m_collapseBtn = new QPushButton(this);
    m_closeBtn = new QPushButton(this);
    // 加载图标。固定按钮走 applyPinIcon：固定=蓝、未固定=灰，缺素材也能看出状态
    QIcon colIcon = getIcon("collapse.png");
    QIcon clsIcon = getIcon("close.png");
    applyPinIcon(m_pinBtn, false);
    
    if (!colIcon.isNull()) m_collapseBtn->setIcon(colIcon);
    else m_collapseBtn->setText(QStringLiteral("➖"));
    
    if (!clsIcon.isNull()) m_closeBtn->setIcon(clsIcon);
    else m_closeBtn->setText(QStringLiteral("✖"));
    m_pinBtn->setIconSize(QSize(16, 16));
    m_collapseBtn->setIconSize(QSize(16, 16));
    m_closeBtn->setIconSize(QSize(16, 16));
    // 换成图标之后按钮上没有文字了，tooltip 就是唯一的说明
    m_pinBtn->setToolTip(QStringLiteral("固定：保持动作页展开"));
    m_collapseBtn->setToolTip(QStringLiteral("折叠为桌面图标"));
    m_closeBtn->setToolTip(QStringLiteral("关闭动作页"));
    // 按钮通用样式：去边框、悬停反馈
    QString btnBaseStyle = QStringLiteral(
        "QPushButton { border: none; background: transparent; border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px; }"
        "QPushButton:hover { background: #E0E0E0; }"
    );
    m_pinBtn->setStyleSheet(btnBaseStyle);
    m_collapseBtn->setStyleSheet(btnBaseStyle);
    m_closeBtn->setStyleSheet(btnBaseStyle + QStringLiteral("QPushButton:hover { background: #FFCDD2; }"));
    
    m_pinBtn->setFixedSize(24, 24);
    m_collapseBtn->setFixedSize(24, 24);
    m_closeBtn->setFixedSize(24, 24);

    topBar->addWidget(m_titleLabel, 1);
    topBar->addWidget(m_pinBtn);
    topBar->addWidget(m_collapseBtn);
    topBar->addWidget(m_closeBtn);
    layout->addLayout(topBar);

    // 网格区
    m_gridHost = new QWidget(this);
    m_grid = new QGridLayout(m_gridHost);
    m_grid->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    m_grid->setSpacing(4);
    layout->addWidget(m_gridHost, 1);

    // 底部翻页栏
    auto* bottom = new QHBoxLayout();
    m_prevBtn = new QPushButton(this);
    m_nextBtn = new QPushButton(this);
    
    // 新增：新建页按钮
    m_addPageBtn = new QPushButton(QStringLiteral("+"), this);
    m_addPageBtn->setFixedSize(24, 24);
    m_addPageBtn->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral("QPushButton { border: none; background: #EAEAEA; border-radius: 12px; color: #555; font-weight: bold; } QPushButton:hover { background: #D0D0D0; }"));
    connect(m_addPageBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ActionPage::addNewPage);
    
    m_pageLabel = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("1 / 1"), this);
    
    QIcon prevIcon = getIcon("prev.png");
    QIcon nextIcon = getIcon("next.png");
    if (!prevIcon.isNull()) m_prevBtn->setIcon(prevIcon);
    else m_prevBtn->setText(QStringLiteral("◀"));
    
    if (!nextIcon.isNull()) m_nextBtn->setIcon(nextIcon);
    else m_nextBtn->setText(QStringLiteral("▶"));
    m_prevBtn->setIconSize(QSize(16, 16));
    m_nextBtn->setIconSize(QSize(16, 16));
    
    // 箭头按钮样式（如果有图标，背景透明更清爽；没图标则显示圆形背景）
    QString arrowStyle = prevIcon.isNull() ? 
        QStringLiteral("QPushButton { border: none; background: #EAEAEA; border-radius: 14px; color: #555; font-weight: bold; min-width: 28px; max-width: 28px; min-height: 28px; max-height: 28px; } QPushButton:hover { background: #D0D0D0; } QPushButton:disabled { background: transparent; color: #CCC; }") :
        QStringLiteral("QPushButton { border: none; background: transparent; border-radius: 4px; padding: 4px; } QPushButton:hover { background: #E0E0E0; } QPushButton:disabled { opacity: 0.5; }");
    m_prevBtn->setStyleSheet(arrowStyle);
    m_nextBtn->setStyleSheet(arrowStyle);
    m_prevBtn->setFixedSize(28, 28);
    m_nextBtn->setFixedSize(28, 28);
    m_pageLabel->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral("font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; color: #666;"));
    m_pageLabel->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    
    bottom->addWidget(m_prevBtn);
    bottom->addStretch();
    bottom->addWidget(m_addPageBtn); // 放在页码左边
    bottom->addWidget(m_pageLabel);
    bottom->addStretch();
    bottom->addWidget(m_nextBtn);
    layout->addLayout(bottom);

    mainLayout->addWidget(m_container);

    connect(m_prevBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] { switchPage(-1); });
    connect(m_nextBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] { switchPage(1); });
    connect(m_pinBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ActionPage::togglePin);
    connect(m_collapseBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ActionPage::toggleCollapse);
    connect(m_closeBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QWidget::hide);

    m_collapseTimer = new QTimer(this);
    m_collapseTimer->setSingleShot(true);
    connect(m_collapseTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] {
        if (!m_isPinned && !m_isCollapsed && underMouse() == false) {
            setCollapsed(true);
        }
    });

    applyTheme(); // 构造完成后应用配置的尺寸与样式
}

void ActionPage::showAtCursor(const QString& scene) {
    m_scene = scene;
    // 自己（Huan.exe）和资源管理器都算"没有明确场景"，退回 global。
    // exe 名走 selfExeName()，以后再改名也不用回来动这一行。
    if (m_scene.compare(AppConstants::selfExeName(), Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0 ||
        m_scene == QLatin1String("explorer.exe")) {
        m_scene = QStringLiteral("global");
    }

    m_currentPage = 0;
    m_previousHwnd = reinterpret_cast<quintptr>(GetForegroundWindow());
    m_titleLabel->setText(QStringLiteral("场景: %1").arg(m_scene));
    
    refresh();
    
    if (m_isCollapsed) {
        setCollapsed(false);
    }

    if (!isVisible()) {
        const QPoint cursor = QCursor::pos();
        QPoint target = cursor + QPoint(16, 16);
        if (QScreen* screen = QGuiApplication::screenAt(cursor)) {
            const QRect available = screen->availableGeometry();
            target.setX(qBound(available.left(), target.x(), available.right() - width()));
            target.setY(qBound(available.top(), target.y(), available.bottom() - height()));
        }
        move(target);
        show();
    }
    raise();
    activateWindow();
}

void ActionPage::mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent* event) {
    if (event->button() == Qt::LeftButton) {
        m_isDragging = true;
        m_dragPosition = event->globalPosition().toPoint() - frameGeometry().topLeft();
        event->accept();
    }
}

void ActionPage::mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent* event) {
    if (m_isDragging && (event->buttons() & Qt::LeftButton)) {
        move(event->globalPosition().toPoint() - m_dragPosition);
        event->accept();
    }
}

void ActionPage::mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent* event) {
    if (event->button() == Qt::LeftButton) {
        m_isDragging = false;
    }
}

void ActionPage::togglePin() {
    m_isPinned = !m_isPinned;
    // 原来这里靠 setStyleSheet 改 color 表达"已固定"，但按钮显示的是 QIcon，
    // color 只对文字生效 —— 图标一直没变过。改成直接换染色的 pixmap。
    applyPinIcon(m_pinBtn, m_isPinned);
    m_pinBtn->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral(
        "QPushButton { border: none; background: transparent; border-radius: 4px; padding: 2px; }"
        "QPushButton:hover { background: #E0E0E0; }"));
    if (m_isPinned) {
        m_collapseTimer->stop();
        setCollapsed(false);
    }
}

void ActionPage::toggleCollapse() {
    setCollapsed(!m_isCollapsed);
}

void ActionPage::setCollapsed(bool collapsed) {
    m_isCollapsed = collapsed;
    if (m_isCollapsed) {
        setFixedSize(kCollapsedSize, kCollapsedSize); // 折叠时保持固定小尺寸
        m_container->hide();
        m_logoLabel->show();
    } else {
        int width = AppConfig::instance().intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.width"), 500, 300, 1920);
        int height = AppConfig::instance().intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.height"), 680, 300, 1080);
        setFixedSize(width, height);
        m_logoLabel->hide();
        m_container->show();
    }
}

void ActionPage::applyTheme() {
    const AppConfig& config = AppConfig::instance();
    
    // 1. 读取并应用面板大小
    int width = config.intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.width"), 500, 300, 1920);
    int height = config.intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.height"), 680, 300, 1080);
    if (!m_isCollapsed) {
        setFixedSize(width, height);
    }
    // 2. 读取颜色
    QString bgColor = config.value(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.bg_color"), "#F8F9FA");
    int radius = config.intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.radius"), 6, 0, 30);
    // 3. 应用主面板样式
    m_container->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral(
        "QWidget#ActionPageMain { background-color: %1; border-radius: %2px; border: 1px solid #D0D0D0; }"
        "QLabel { background: transparent; }"
    ).arg(bgColor).arg(radius));
    // 4. 刷新网格以应用按钮样式
    refresh();
}

void ActionPage::enterEvent(QEnterEvent* event) {
    m_collapseTimer->stop();
    if (m_isCollapsed) {
        setCollapsed(false);
    }
    QWidget::enterEvent(event);
}

void ActionPage::leaveEvent(QEvent* event) {
    if (!m_isPinned && isVisible()) {
        m_collapseTimer->start(1500);
    }
    QWidget::leaveEvent(event);
}

void ActionPage::refresh() {
    if (m_gridHost->layout()) {
        QLayoutItem* item;
        while ((item = m_grid->takeAt(0)) != nullptr) {
            if (item->widget()) item->widget()->deleteLater();
            delete item;
        }
    }

    // 1. 检查场景回退
    QString targetScene = m_scene;
    QMap<int, int> bindings = ActionRepository::getPageBindings(targetScene, m_currentPage);
    
    if (bindings.isEmpty() && targetScene != QLatin1String("global")) {
        targetScene = QStringLiteral("global");
        bindings = ActionRepository::getPageBindings(targetScene, m_currentPage);
        m_titleLabel->setText(QStringLiteral("场景: %1 (继承全局)").arg(m_scene));
    } else {
        m_titleLabel->setText(QStringLiteral("场景: %1").arg(m_scene));
    }
    // 2. 紧凑布局控制 (如果配置没自定义，这里把默认间距改小)
    int hSpacing = AppConfig::instance().intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.h_spacing"), 4, 0, 50);
    int vSpacing = AppConfig::instance().intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.v_spacing"), 4, 0, 50);
    
    m_grid->setHorizontalSpacing(hSpacing);
    m_grid->setVerticalSpacing(vSpacing);
    m_grid->setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4); // 边缘保持一点微小留白
    QVector<LibraryAction> library = ActionRepository::getActionLibrary();
    QMap<int, LibraryAction> libMap;
    for (const auto& a : library) libMap.insert(a.id, a);
    // 读取配置的颜色和尺寸
    const AppConfig& config = AppConfig::instance();
    int btnW = config.intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.btn_width"), 100, 50, 300);
    int btnH = config.intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.btn_height"), 70, 40, 200);
    QString btnBg = config.value(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.btn_bg"), "#FFFFFF");
    QString btnHoverBg = config.value(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.btn_hover_bg"), "#eaf3ff");
    QString textColor = config.value(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.text_color"), "#333333");
    int radius = config.intValue(QStringLiteral("ui.actionpage.radius"), 6, 0, 30);
    QString btnStyle = QStringLiteral(
        "QPushButton { text-align: center; border: 1px solid #d0d0d0; border-radius: %1px; "
        "background: %2; color: %3; padding: 2px; } "
        "QPushButton:hover { background: %4; border-color: #0078D7; }"
    ).arg(radius).arg(btnBg, textColor, btnHoverBg);

    for (int i = 0; i < kPageSize; ++i) {
        int r = i / kCols;
        int c = i % kCols;

        const int boundId = bindings.value(i, -1);
        auto* btn = new ActionButton(i, boundId, m_gridHost);
        btn->setMinimumSize(btnW, btnH);
        
        // === 新增：强制按钮尺寸策略，防止被长文字撑大 ===
        btn->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Ignored, QSizePolicy::Ignored);
        // 强制每一列平分宽度
        m_grid->setColumnStretch(c, 1); 
        
        btn->setStyleSheet(btnStyle);

        if (bindings.contains(i)) {
            int actionId = bindings.value(i);
            if (libMap.contains(actionId)) {
                LibraryAction act = libMap.value(actionId);

                // 优化：使用 QFontMetrics 截断过长的文字，防止换行破坏排版
                QFontMetrics metrics(btn->font());
                QString displayText = QStringLiteral("%1\n(%2)").arg(act.name, typeName(act.type));
                // 你可以根据需要调整截断的宽度 (btnW - 10)
                // btn->setText(metrics.elidedText(displayText, Qt::ElideRight, btnW - 10)); 
                // 但如果希望保留两行显示，直接 setText 也可以，因为设置了 Ignored，按钮不会被撑大，只会内部显示省略号
                btn->setText(displayText);
                
                connect(btn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, act] {
                    Action a;
                    a.id = act.id; // 不带 id 的话引擎那边统计不到，点了也白点
                    a.slot = -1;
                    a.name = act.name;
                    a.type = act.type;
                    a.payload = act.payload;
                    execute(a);
                });
            }
        } else {
            btn->setText(QStringLiteral("空白"));
            btn->setStyleSheet(btnStyle + "QPushButton { color: #aaa; background: #FAFAFA; }");
        }

        btn->onActionDropped = [this](int idx, int actionId) {
            ActionRepository::bindActionToPage(m_currentPage, idx, m_scene, actionId);
            refresh();
        };

        // 页内拖拽交换：源格子与目标格子对调，任一端为空则解绑该端
        btn->onActionSwapped = [this](int srcIdx, int dstIdx) {
            if (srcIdx == dstIdx) return;
            const QMap<int, int> current = ActionRepository::getPageBindings(m_scene, m_currentPage);
            const int srcActionId = current.value(srcIdx, -1);
            const int dstActionId = current.value(dstIdx, -1);

            if (dstActionId > 0) {
                ActionRepository::bindActionToPage(m_currentPage, srcIdx, m_scene, dstActionId);
            } else {
                ActionRepository::unbindPageSlot(m_currentPage, srcIdx, m_scene);
            }
            if (srcActionId > 0) {
                ActionRepository::bindActionToPage(m_currentPage, dstIdx, m_scene, srcActionId);
            } else {
                ActionRepository::unbindPageSlot(m_currentPage, dstIdx, m_scene);
            }
            refresh();
            emit configSaved();
        };
        
        m_grid->addWidget(btn, r, c);
    }
    
    for (int r = 0; r < kRows; ++r) {
        m_grid->setRowStretch(r, 1);
    }

    // 3. 页码显示 "1 / N"
    int totalPages = ActionRepository::getScenePageCount(targetScene);
    if (m_currentPage >= totalPages) {
        m_currentPage = totalPages - 1;
    }
    m_pageLabel->setText(QStringLiteral("%1 / %2").arg(m_currentPage + 1).arg(totalPages));
    m_prevBtn->setEnabled(m_currentPage > 0);
    m_nextBtn->setEnabled(m_currentPage + 1 < totalPages);
}


void ActionPage::addNewPage() {
    // 防御：当前场景为空时回退到 global，防止底层 setScenePageCount 静默失败
    if (m_scene.isEmpty()) {
        m_scene = QStringLiteral("global");
    }
    int newPageIndex = showNewPageDialog(this, m_scene);
    if (newPageIndex >= 0) {
        m_currentPage = newPageIndex;
        refresh();
        emit configSaved();
    }
}

void ActionPage::switchPage(int delta) {
    int newPage = m_currentPage + delta;
    if (newPage >= 0) {
        m_currentPage = newPage;
        refresh();
    }
}

void ActionPage::execute(const Action& action) {
    const quintptr target = m_previousHwnd;
    const QString scene = m_scene;

    // 关键：无论是否置顶，执行前都必须隐藏自己，否则按键会发给面板自己
    bool wasPinned = m_isPinned;
    hide(); 

    if (target != 0) {
        Win32Utils::forceForegroundWindow(reinterpret_cast<HWND>(target));
    }

    QTimer::singleShot(150, this, [this, action, scene, wasPinned] {
        ActionEngine::executeAction(action, scene);
        // 如果之前是置顶状态，执行完后重新显示出来
        if (wasPinned && m_isPinned) {
            show();
            raise();
        }
    });
}

void ActionPage::closeEvent(QCloseEvent* event) {
    hide();
    event->ignore();
}

void ActionPage::keyPressEvent(QKeyEvent* event) {
    if (event->key() == Qt::Key_Escape) {
        hide();
        return;
    }
    QWidget::keyPressEvent(event);
}

};

namespace {

constexpr char kActionMime[] = "application/x-huan-action-id";
// 动作页格子间拖拽交换：携带源格子索引（与上面的 actionId 同时写入同一份 MimeData）
constexpr char kPageIndexMime[] = "application/x-huan-page-index";
// 轮盘内槽位拖拽交换：携带源槽位号
constexpr char kWheelSlotMime[] = "application/x-huan-wheel-slot";

QString actionTypeName(int type) {
    switch (type) {
    case 0: return QStringLiteral("按键组合");
    case 1: return QStringLiteral("运行程序/打开链接");
    case 2: return QStringLiteral("剪贴板转换");
    case 3: return QStringLiteral("外部脚本");
    case 4: return QStringLiteral("多步流水线");
    case 5: return QStringLiteral("输入文本");
    default: return QString();
    }
}

QString quotedPath(const QString& file) {
    return QStringLiteral("\"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(file));
}

bool isClipboardOp(const QString& text) {
    const QString operation = text.trimmed().toLower();
    return operation == QLatin1String("upper") || operation == QLatin1String("lower") ||
           operation == QLatin1String("title") || operation == QLatin1String("trim") ||
           operation == QLatin1String("reverse");
}

// 只接受从动作库拖来的数据。
class SlotTable : public QTableWidget {
public:
    explicit SlotTable(QWidget* parent = nullptr) : QTableWidget(parent) {}

    std::function<void(int slot, int actionId)> onActionDropped;

protected:
    void dragEnterEvent(QDragEnterEvent* event) override {
        if (event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kActionMime)) {
            event->acceptProposedAction();
            return;
        }
        QTableWidget::dragEnterEvent(event);
    }

    void dragMoveEvent(QDragMoveEvent* event) override {
        if (event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kActionMime)) {
            event->acceptProposedAction();
            return;
        }
        QTableWidget::dragMoveEvent(event);
    }

    void dropEvent(QDropEvent* event) override {
        if (!event->mimeData()->hasFormat(kActionMime)) {
            QTableWidget::dropEvent(event);
            return;
        }

        const int actionId = event->mimeData()->data(kActionMime).toInt();
        if (actionId <= 0) {
            event->ignore();
            return;
        }

        const int row = rowAt(event->position().toPoint().y());
        if (row >= 0 && row < rowCount() && onActionDropped) {
            onActionDropped(row, actionId);
            event->acceptProposedAction();
            return;
        }
        event->ignore();
    }
};

// 拖动时把动作 id 写进自定义 MIME。
class LibraryTable : public QTableWidget {
public:
    explicit LibraryTable(QWidget* parent = nullptr) : QTableWidget(parent) {}

protected:
    QMimeData* mimeData(const QList<QTableWidgetItem*>& items) const override {
        QMimeData* mime = QTableWidget::mimeData(items);
        if (!items.isEmpty()) {
            const int row = items.first()->row();
            if (QTableWidgetItem* nameItem = item(row, 0)) {
                const int actionId = nameItem->data(Qt::UserRole).toInt();
                mime->setData(kActionMime, QByteArray::number(actionId));
            }
        }
        return mime;
    }
};

} // namespace（临时关闭，让 KeyRecorderWidget 位于全局作用域，与 MainWindow.h 的前向声明一致）

class KeyRecorderWidget : public QWidget {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit KeyRecorderWidget(QWidget* parent = nullptr) : QWidget(parent) {
        auto* layout = new QHBoxLayout(this);
        layout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        layout->setSpacing(4);
        m_edit = new QLineEdit(this);
        m_edit->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("输入按键组合或点击录制..."));
        
        m_recordBtn = new QPushButton(QStringLiteral("录制"), this);
        m_recordBtn->setCheckable(true);
        m_recordBtn->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral(
            "QPushButton { padding: 4px 8px; } "
            "QPushButton:checked { background-color: #FFCDD2; color: red; font-weight: bold; }"
        ));
        layout->addWidget(m_edit, 1);
        layout->addWidget(m_recordBtn);
        // 安装事件过滤器到自身：grabKeyboard 后键盘事件送到本控件，先经过 eventFilter
        installEventFilter(this);
        // 点击录制按钮
        connect(m_recordBtn, &QPushButton::toggled, this, [this](bool checked) {
            if (checked) {
                m_edit->setReadOnly(true);
                m_edit->setPlaceholderText(
                    QStringLiteral("请按下组合键（Win 组合键可能被系统拦截，可手动输入）"));
                m_edit->setText(QString());
                m_lastMods.clear();
                m_pressedKeys.clear();
                m_recordBtn->setText(QStringLiteral("停止"));
                // 抓取键盘焦点，开始拦截
                this->grabKeyboard();
                this->setFocus();
            } else {
                stopRecording();
            }
        });
    }
    // === 新增：暴露当前是否处于录制状态 ===
    bool isRecording() const { return m_recordBtn->isChecked(); }
    
    // 暴露内部 QLineEdit，方便其他地方调用
    QLineEdit* lineEdit() const { return m_edit; }
    QString text() const { return m_edit->text(); }
    void setText(const QString& text) { m_edit->setText(text); }
    // 供调用方按动作类型提示（转发给内部输入框）
    void setPlaceholderText(const QString& text) { m_edit->setPlaceholderText(text); }
protected:
    // 拦截所有键盘事件，包括系统快捷键
    bool eventFilter(QObject* watched, QEvent* event) override {
        if (!m_recordBtn->isChecked()) {
            return QWidget::eventFilter(watched, event);
        }
        if (event->type() == QEvent::KeyPress) {
            QKeyEvent* keyEvent = static_cast<QKeyEvent*>(event);
            int key = keyEvent->key();
            // 修饰键（Ctrl/Shift/Alt/Win）：只记录按下状态，绝不写进文本。
            // 主键按下时统一从 modifiers 里取修饰键，这样不会出现 "Win+Ctrl+Win+A" 的重复拼接
            if (key == Qt::Key_Control || key == Qt::Key_Shift || key == Qt::Key_Alt || key == Qt::Key_Meta) {
                if (key == Qt::Key_Meta && !m_pressedKeys.contains(key)) {
                    QToolTip::showText(mapToGlobal(QPoint(0, height())),
                                       QStringLiteral("Win 组合键可能被 Windows 拦截（如 Win+D / Win+E）。\n"
                                                      "若录制无反应，请停止录制后手动输入。"),
                                       this, QRect(), 4000);
                }
                m_pressedKeys.insert(key);
                keyEvent->accept();
                return true;
            }
            // 如果这个键已经在按下集合里，说明是长按产生的系统重复事件，直接拦截丢弃
            if (m_pressedKeys.contains(key)) {
                keyEvent->accept();
                return true;
            }
            m_pressedKeys.insert(key); // 记录这个键已经按下了
            // === 组装当前这一次的组合键 ===
            QStringList parts;
            if (keyEvent->modifiers() & Qt::ControlModifier) parts << QStringLiteral("Ctrl");
            if (keyEvent->modifiers() & Qt::ShiftModifier) parts << QStringLiteral("Shift");
            if (keyEvent->modifiers() & Qt::AltModifier) parts << QStringLiteral("Alt");
            if (keyEvent->modifiers() & Qt::MetaModifier) parts << QStringLiteral("Win");
            QString keyName;
            if (key >= Qt::Key_A && key <= Qt::Key_Z) keyName = QChar(key);
            else if (key >= Qt::Key_0 && key <= Qt::Key_9) keyName = QChar(key);
            else if (key >= Qt::Key_F1 && key <= Qt::Key_F24) keyName = QStringLiteral("F%1").arg(key - Qt::Key_F1 + 1);
            else if (key == Qt::Key_Space) keyName = QStringLiteral("Space");
            else if (key == Qt::Key_Return || key == Qt::Key_Enter) keyName = QStringLiteral("Enter");
            else if (key == Qt::Key_Escape) keyName = QStringLiteral("Esc");
            else if (key == Qt::Key_Plus) keyName = QStringLiteral("+");
            else {
                keyName = QKeySequence(key).toString(QKeySequence::PortableText);
                if (keyName.isEmpty() && keyEvent->nativeVirtualKey() != 0) {
                    keyName = QStringLiteral("VK_%1").arg(keyEvent->nativeVirtualKey());
                }
            }
            if (keyName.isEmpty()) {
                keyEvent->accept();
                return true;
            }
            parts << keyName;
            const QString comboStr = parts.join(QLatin1Char('+'));
            const QString modsStr = QStringList(parts.mid(0, parts.size() - 1)).join(QLatin1Char('+'));
            const QString currentText = m_edit->text();
            if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
                m_edit->setText(comboStr);
                m_lastMods = modsStr;
            } else if (!modsStr.isEmpty() && modsStr == m_lastMods) {
                // 同一组修饰键下继续按键：Ctrl+A → Ctrl+A+C
                m_edit->setText(currentText + QLatin1Char('+') + keyName);
            } else {
                m_edit->setText(currentText + QLatin1Char('+') + comboStr);
                m_lastMods = modsStr;
            }
            keyEvent->accept();
            return true; // 吞噬按下事件，防止发出声音或触发焦点切换
        } 
        else if (event->type() == QEvent::KeyRelease) {
            QKeyEvent* keyEvent = static_cast<QKeyEvent*>(event);
            int key = keyEvent->key();
            // 修饰键松开且没有别的键按着 → 本次录制结束
            if (key == Qt::Key_Control || key == Qt::Key_Shift || key == Qt::Key_Alt || key == Qt::Key_Meta) {
                m_pressedKeys.remove(key);
                if (m_pressedKeys.isEmpty()) {
                    m_recordBtn->setChecked(false); // 触发停止录制
                }
            } else {
                // 主键松开，从集合中移除，允许下次重新按下
                m_pressedKeys.remove(key);
            }
            keyEvent->accept();
            return true;
        }
        return QWidget::eventFilter(watched, event);
    }
    void keyPressEvent(QKeyEvent* event) override {
        if (m_recordBtn->isChecked()) {
            // 关键：拦截 Alt+F4 等窗口关闭事件
            if (event->key() == Qt::Key_F4 && (event->modifiers() & Qt::AltModifier)) {
                event->accept();
                return;
            }
        }
        QWidget::keyPressEvent(event);
    }
private:
    void stopRecording() {
        m_edit->setReadOnly(false);
        m_edit->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("输入按键组合或点击录制..."));
        m_recordBtn->setText(QStringLiteral("录制"));
        m_pressedKeys.clear(); // 关键：每次停止录制必须清空状态
        m_lastMods.clear();
        this->releaseKeyboard();
    }
    QLineEdit* m_edit = nullptr;
    QPushButton* m_recordBtn = nullptr;
    QSet<int> m_pressedKeys; // 新增：记录当前按下的主键，用于防抖
    QString m_lastMods; // 上一段录制使用的修饰键组合（Ctrl+Shift 等），用于判断修饰键是否变化
};

namespace {

class ActionEditDialog : public QDialog {
public:
    ActionEditDialog(QWidget* parent, const LibraryAction& initial, bool isNew)
        : QDialog(parent), m_action(initial) {
        setWindowTitle(isNew ? QStringLiteral("新增动作") : QStringLiteral("编辑动作"));
        resize(540, 230);

        auto* layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
        auto* form = new QFormLayout();

        m_name = new QLineEdit(initial.name, this);
        m_name->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("显示在轮盘/动作页上的名字"));
        form->addRow(QStringLiteral("名字:"), m_name);

        m_type = new QComboBox(this);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("按键组合"), 0);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("运行程序/打开链接"), 1);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("剪贴板转换"), 2);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("外部脚本"), 3);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("多步流水线"), 4);
        m_type->addItem(QStringLiteral("输入文本"), 5);
        form->addRow(QStringLiteral("类型:"), m_type);

        auto* payloadRow = new QHBoxLayout();
        m_payload = new KeyRecorderWidget(this);
        m_payload->setText(initial.payload);
        m_browse = new QToolButton(this);
        m_browse->setText(QStringLiteral("..."));
        m_browse->setFixedWidth(26);
        payloadRow->addWidget(m_payload, 1);
        payloadRow->addWidget(m_browse);
        form->addRow(QStringLiteral("参数:"), payloadRow);
        // 固定说明：系统级快捷键录不到时，用户知道这是系统限制而不是软件出问题
        auto* hotkeyHint = new QLabel(
            QStringLiteral("提示：部分系统级快捷键（如 Win 键开头的组合、Alt+F4）无法直接录制，"
                           "请停止录制后在输入框中手动输入。"),
            this);
        hotkeyHint->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral("color: #888888; font-size: 11px;"));
        hotkeyHint->setWordWrap(true);
        form->addRow(hotkeyHint);

        layout->addLayout(form);

        auto* buttons = new QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox::Ok | QDialogButtonBox::Cancel, this);
        layout->addWidget(buttons);

        connect(buttons, &QDialogButtonBox::accepted, this, [this] { tryAccept(); });
        connect(buttons, &QDialogButtonBox::rejected, this, &QDialog::reject);
        connect(m_type, &QComboBox::currentIndexChanged, this, [this](int) { updatePlaceholder(); });
        connect(m_browse, &QToolButton::clicked, this, [this] { browse(); });

        const int index = m_type->findData(initial.type >= 0 ? initial.type : 0);
        m_type->setCurrentIndex(index >= 0 ? index : 0);
        updatePlaceholder();
    }

    LibraryAction result() const {
        LibraryAction action = m_action;
        action.name = m_name->text().trimmed();
        action.type = m_type->currentData().toInt();
        action.payload = m_payload->text().trimmed();
        return action;
    }

protected:
    // === 新增：拦截窗口关闭事件 ===
    bool event(QEvent* e) override {
        // 如果当前正在录制，且系统尝试关闭窗口（例如按下 Alt+F4）
        if (m_payload && m_payload->isRecording() && e->type() == QEvent::Close) {
            e->ignore(); // 忽略关闭请求
            return true; // 拦截事件，不让它继续传递
        }
        return QDialog::event(e);
    }
    // ==========================
    void closeEvent(QCloseEvent* event) override {
        // 双保险：重写 closeEvent
        if (m_payload && m_payload->isRecording()) {
            event->ignore();
            return;
        }
        QDialog::closeEvent(event);
    }

private:
    void updatePlaceholder() {
        switch (m_type->currentData().toInt()) {
        case 0:
            m_payload->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("例如 Ctrl+Shift+Esc"));
            break;
        case 1:
            m_payload->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("例如 C:\\Windows\\notepad.exe 或 https://..."));
            break;
        case 2:
            m_payload->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("upper / lower / title / trim / reverse"));
            break;
        case 3:
            m_payload->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("例如 python \"C:\\scripts\\x.py\""));
            break;
        case 4:
            m_payload->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("JSON 数组，点 ... 编辑"));
            break;
        case 5:
            m_payload->setPlaceholderText(QStringLiteral("要输入的文本，支持变量"));
            break;
        default:
            break;
        }
    }

    void browse() {
        const int type = m_type->currentData().toInt();
        if (type == 1) {
            const QString file = QFileDialog::getOpenFileName(
                this, QStringLiteral("选择程序"), QString(),
                QStringLiteral("可执行文件 (*.exe);;所有文件 (*.*)"));
            if (!file.isEmpty()) m_payload->setText(quotedPath(file));
            return;
        }
        if (type == 3) {
            const QString file = QFileDialog::getOpenFileName(
                this, QStringLiteral("选择脚本"), QString(),
                QStringLiteral("脚本文件 (*.py *.js *.mjs *.ps1 *.bat *.cmd *.vbs *.exe);;所有文件 (*.*)"));
            if (!file.isEmpty()) m_payload->setText(quotedPath(file));
            return;
        }
        if (type == 4) {
            bool ok = false;
            const QString text = QInputDialog::getMultiLineText(
                this, QStringLiteral("多步流水线"),
                QStringLiteral("JSON 数组。示例：\n"
                               "[{\"type\":0,\"payload\":\"Ctrl+C\"},{\"wait\":120},"
                               "{\"type\":5,\"payload\":\"{clipboard}\"}]\n\n"
                               "步骤类型：key/launch/clipboard/script/pipeline/text，"
                               "也可以用数字 0-5。wait 表示等待毫秒数。"),
                m_payload->text(), &ok);
            if (!ok) return;

            QJsonParseError error;
            const QJsonDocument document = QJsonDocument::fromJson(text.toUtf8(), &error);
            if (error.error != QJsonParseError::NoError) {
                QMessageBox::warning(this, QStringLiteral("JSON 格式错误"),
                                     QStringLiteral("%1（位置 %2）")
                                         .arg(error.errorString())
                                         .arg(error.offset));
                return;
            }
            m_payload->setText(QString::fromUtf8(document.toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact)));
            return;
        }
        if (type == 2) {
            QMessageBox::information(
                this, QStringLiteral("剪贴板转换"),
                QStringLiteral("参数只填操作名：\n\nupper 转大写\nlower 转小写\n"
                               "title 首字母大写\ntrim 去掉首尾空白\nreverse 反转文字"));
            return;
        }
        if (type == 5) {
            QMessageBox::information(
                this, QStringLiteral("输入文本"),
                QStringLiteral("会以 Unicode 方式逐字输入，不受输入法影响。\n\n"
                               "可以使用变量：{clipboard} {selected} {scene} {date} {time} {uuid} "
                               "{newline} {env:NAME}"));
            return;
        }

        QMessageBox::information(
            this, QStringLiteral("按键组合"),
            QStringLiteral("支持：Ctrl / Shift / Alt / Win 与任意主键组合。\n\n"
                           "示例：\nCtrl+Shift+Esc\nAlt+F4\nWin+Shift+S\nMediaPlayPause\n"
                           "Ctrl+Left\nNumpadEnter\nCtrl++（Ctrl 加加号）\n\n"
                           "注意：Ctrl+Alt+Del 是系统安全序列，无法模拟。"));
    }

    void tryAccept() {
        const LibraryAction action = result();
        if (action.name.isEmpty()) {
            QMessageBox::warning(this, QStringLiteral("名字为空"),
                                 QStringLiteral("请给动作起一个名字。"));
            return;
        }

        if (action.type == 0) {
            const KeyChord chord = Win32Utils::parseKeySequence(action.payload);
            if (!chord.valid) {
                QMessageBox::warning(this, QStringLiteral("按键无效"), chord.error);
                return;
            }
        } else if (action.type == 1 || action.type == 3 || action.type == 5) {
            if (action.payload.isEmpty()) {
                QMessageBox::warning(this, QStringLiteral("参数为空"),
                                     QStringLiteral("这个动作类型需要填写参数。"));
                return;
            }
        } else if (action.type == 2) {
            if (!isClipboardOp(action.payload)) {
                QMessageBox::warning(this, QStringLiteral("剪贴板操作无效"),
                                     QStringLiteral("只能填 upper / lower / title / trim / reverse。"));
                return;
            }
        } else if (action.type == 4) {
            QJsonParseError error;
            const QJsonDocument document = QJsonDocument::fromJson(action.payload.toUtf8(), &error);
            if (error.error != QJsonParseError::NoError ||
                !(document.isArray() || document.isObject())) {
                QMessageBox::warning(this, QStringLiteral("流水线 JSON 无效"), error.errorString());
                return;
            }
        }

        accept();
    }

    LibraryAction m_action;
    QLineEdit* m_name = nullptr;
