        add("LEFTBRACKET", VK_OEM_4);
        add("RBRACKET", VK_OEM_6);
        add("RIGHTBRACKET", VK_OEM_6);
        add("BACKSLASH", VK_OEM_5);
        add("SEMICOLON", VK_OEM_1);
        add("QUOTE", VK_OEM_7);
        add("APOSTROPHE", VK_OEM_7);
        add("COMMA", VK_OEM_COMMA);
        add("PERIOD", VK_OEM_PERIOD);
        add("DOT", VK_OEM_PERIOD);
        add("SLASH", VK_OEM_2);

        return m;
    }();
    return map;
}

void addModifier(QVector<KeyInput>& modifiers, const KeyInput& modifier) {
    for (const KeyInput& existing : modifiers) {
        if (existing.vk == modifier.vk) return;
    }
    modifiers.append(modifier);
}

bool addCharacterKey(QChar ch, KeyInput& out, QVector<KeyInput>& modifiers, QString& error) {
    const ushort code = ch.unicode();
    if (code < 32 || code > 126) {
        error = QStringLiteral("暂不支持字符“%1”。发送中文/任意文本请使用“输入文本”动作。").arg(ch);
        return false;
    }

    if (code >= 'a' && code <= 'z') {
        out = {static_cast<WORD>(code - ('a' - 'A')), false};
        return true;
    }
    if (code >= 'A' && code <= 'Z') {
        out = {static_cast<WORD>(code), false};
        return true;
    }
    if (code >= '0' && code <= '9') {
        out = {static_cast<WORD>(code), false};
        return true;
    }

    const SHORT scan = VkKeyScanW(static_cast<WCHAR>(code));
    if (scan == -1) {
        error = QStringLiteral("当前键盘布局无法输入字符“%1”。").arg(ch);
        return false;
    }

    out.vk = static_cast<WORD>(LOBYTE(scan));
    out.extended = false;

    const BYTE state = HIBYTE(scan);
    if (state & 1) addModifier(modifiers, {VK_SHIFT, false});
    if (state & 2) addModifier(modifiers, {VK_CONTROL, false});
    if (state & 4) addModifier(modifiers, {VK_MENU, false});
    return true;
}

} // namespace

KeyChord Win32Utils::parseKeySequence(const QString& sequence) {
    KeyChord chord;

    const QString text = sequence.trimmed();
    if (text.isEmpty()) {
        chord.error = QStringLiteral("按键序列为空");
        return chord;
    }

    // 按 '+' 切分，同时把末尾的 '+' 当作“加号键”，例如 Ctrl++。
    QStringList tokens;
    int start = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < text.size(); ++i) {
        if (text.at(i) != QLatin1Char('+')) continue;

        const QString token = text.mid(start, i - start).trimmed();
        if (!token.isEmpty()) tokens.append(token);
        if (i == text.size() - 1) tokens.append(QStringLiteral("+"));

        start = i + 1;
    }
    if (start < text.size()) {
        const QString tail = text.mid(start).trimmed();
        if (!tail.isEmpty()) tokens.append(tail);
    }

    for (const QString& token : tokens) {
        const QString normalized = normalizedToken(token);

        KeyInput modifier;
        if (modifierToken(normalized, modifier)) {
            addModifier(chord.modifiers, modifier);
            continue;
        }

        // === 修改点：移除只能有一个主键的报错，改为追加 ===
        KeyInput newKey;
        // F1 - F24
        if (normalized.startsWith(QLatin1Char('F')) && normalized.size() <= 3) {
            bool ok = false;
            const int number = normalized.mid(1).toInt(&ok);
            if (ok && number >= 1 && number <= 24) {
                newKey = {static_cast<WORD>(VK_F1 + number - 1), false};
                chord.keys.append(newKey);
                continue;
            }
        }

        const auto it = namedKeys().constFind(normalized);
        if (!normalized.isEmpty() && it != namedKeys().constEnd()) {
            newKey = it.value();
            chord.keys.append(newKey);
            continue;
        }

        if (token == QLatin1String("+") || normalized == QLatin1String("PLUS")) {
            if (!addCharacterKey(QLatin1Char('+'), newKey, chord.modifiers, chord.error)) {
                return chord;
            }
            chord.keys.append(newKey);
            continue;
        }

        if (token.size() == 1) {
            if (!addCharacterKey(token.at(0), newKey, chord.modifiers, chord.error)) {
                return chord;
            }
            chord.keys.append(newKey);
            continue;
        }

        chord.error = QStringLiteral("未知按键“%1”。可用示例：Ctrl+Shift+Esc、Alt+F4、Win+Space、MediaPlayPause、Ctrl+Left").arg(token);
        return chord;
    }

    if (chord.keys.isEmpty()) { // 修改为检查列表
        chord.error = QStringLiteral("缺少主键，不能只发送修饰键");
        return chord;
    }
    // 顺便给旧的 key 赋个值，防止其他地方崩溃
    chord.key = chord.keys.first();

    auto hasModifier = [&chord](WORD vk) {
        for (const KeyInput& modifier : chord.modifiers) {
            if (modifier.vk == vk) return true;
        }
        return false;
    };

    // Ctrl+Alt+Del 由 Windows 安全机制接管，普通进程无法通过 SendInput 触发。
    bool hasDel = false;
    for (const auto& k : chord.keys) {
        if (k.vk == VK_DELETE) hasDel = true;
    }
    if (hasDel && hasModifier(VK_CONTROL) && hasModifier(VK_MENU)) {
        chord.error = QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Alt+Del 是 Windows 安全注意序列，任何普通程序都无法模拟");
        return chord;
    }

    chord.valid = true;
    return chord;
}

bool Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(const QString& sequence, QString* error) {
    const KeyChord chord = parseKeySequence(sequence);
    if (!chord.valid) {
        if (error) *error = chord.error;
        return false;
    }

    // 先按下修饰键，给目标程序留出识别组合键的时间。
    QVector<INPUT> modifiersDown;
    modifiersDown.reserve(chord.modifiers.size());
    for (const KeyInput& modifier : chord.modifiers) {
        modifiersDown.append(makeKeyInput(modifier.vk, modifier.extended, false));
        g_injectedModifiers.insert(modifier.vk);
    }

    QVector<INPUT> modifiersUp;
    modifiersUp.reserve(chord.modifiers.size());
    for (int i = chord.modifiers.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        modifiersUp.append(makeKeyInput(chord.modifiers[i].vk, chord.modifiers[i].extended, true));
    }

    if (!modifiersDown.isEmpty()) {
        if (!flushInput(modifiersDown, error)) {
            clearModifiers();
            return false;
        }
        Sleep(12);
    }

    // === 修改点：依次按下所有主键 ===
    for (const KeyInput& key : chord.keys) {
        const QVector<INPUT> mainDown{makeKeyInput(key.vk, key.extended, false)};
        if (!flushInput(mainDown, error)) {
            clearModifiers();
            return false;
        }
        Sleep(10); // 给每个按键一点间隔
    }
    Sleep(20);
    // === 修改点：依次松开所有主键（逆序） ===
    for (int i = chord.keys.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        const KeyInput& key = chord.keys[i];
        const QVector<INPUT> mainUp{makeKeyInput(key.vk, key.extended, true)};
        if (!flushInput(mainUp, error)) {
            clearModifiers();
            return false;
        }
        Sleep(10);
    }

    if (!chord.modifiers.isEmpty()) {
        Sleep(12);
        if (!flushInput(modifiersUp, error)) {
            clearModifiers();
            return false;
        }
        for (const KeyInput& modifier : chord.modifiers) {
            g_injectedModifiers.remove(modifier.vk);
        }
    }

    return true;
}

bool Win32Utils::sendText(const QString& text, QString* error) {
    if (text.isEmpty()) return true;

    QVector<INPUT> batch;
    batch.reserve(512);

    auto flush = [&batch, error]() -> bool {
        if (batch.isEmpty()) return true;
        const bool ok = flushInput(batch, error);
        batch.clear();
        return ok;
    };

    auto appendKey = [&batch](WORD vk, bool extended) {
        batch.append(makeKeyInput(vk, extended, false));
        batch.append(makeKeyInput(vk, extended, true));
    };

    for (QChar ch : text) {
        if (ch == QLatin1Char('\r')) continue;

        if (ch == QLatin1Char('\n')) {
            appendKey(VK_RETURN, false);
        } else if (ch == QLatin1Char('\t')) {
            appendKey(VK_TAB, false);
        } else {
            INPUT down{};
            down.type = INPUT_KEYBOARD;
            down.ki.wScan = ch.unicode();
            down.ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_UNICODE;

            INPUT up = down;
            up.ki.dwFlags |= KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;

            batch.append(down);
            batch.append(up);
        }

        if (batch.size() >= 512 && !flush()) return false;
    }

    return flush();
}

void Win32Utils::sendKey(WORD vk, bool withCtrl) {
    if (withCtrl) {
        const QVector<INPUT> ctrlDown{makeKeyInput(VK_CONTROL, false, false)};
        QString error;
        if (!flushInput(ctrlDown, &error)) {
            qWarning().noquote() << error;
            return;
        }
        g_injectedModifiers.insert(VK_CONTROL);
        Sleep(10);
    }

    const QVector<INPUT> keyDown{makeKeyInput(vk, false, false)};
    QString error;
    if (!flushInput(keyDown, &error)) {
        qWarning().noquote() << error;
        clearModifiers();
        return;
    }
    Sleep(35);

    const QVector<INPUT> keyUp{makeKeyInput(vk, false, true)};
    flushInput(keyUp, &error);

    if (withCtrl) {
        Sleep(10);
        const QVector<INPUT> ctrlUp{makeKeyInput(VK_CONTROL, false, true)};
        flushInput(ctrlUp, &error);
        g_injectedModifiers.remove(VK_CONTROL);
    }
}

void Win32Utils::clearModifiers() {
    QVector<INPUT> releases;
    releases.reserve(g_injectedModifiers.size());
    for (WORD vk : g_injectedModifiers) {
        releases.append(makeKeyInput(vk, false, true));
    }

    const QSet<WORD> released = g_injectedModifiers;
    g_injectedModifiers.clear();

    if (!releases.isEmpty()) {
        QString error;
        flushInput(releases, &error);
    }

    // RAlt / RCtrl 在扩展键状态下可能需要单独释放。
    QVector<INPUT> extendedReleases;
    if (released.contains(VK_RMENU)) extendedReleases.append(makeKeyInput(VK_RMENU, true, true));
    if (released.contains(VK_RCONTROL)) extendedReleases.append(makeKeyInput(VK_RCONTROL, true, true));
    if (!extendedReleases.isEmpty()) {
        QString error;
        flushInput(extendedReleases, &error);
    }
}

QString Win32Utils::getClipboardText() {
    if (!openClipboardWithRetry()) return QString();

    const HANDLE data = GetClipboardData(CF_UNICODETEXT);
    if (!data) {
        CloseClipboard();
        return QString();
    }

    const wchar_t* text = static_cast<const wchar_t*>(GlobalLock(data));
    if (!text) {
        CloseClipboard();
        return QString();
    }

    const QString result = QString::fromWCharArray(text);
    GlobalUnlock(data);
    CloseClipboard();
    return result;
}

bool Win32Utils::setClipboardText(const QString& text) {
    if (!openClipboardWithRetry()) return false;

    EmptyClipboard();

    const SIZE_T bytes = (static_cast<SIZE_T>(text.length()) + 1) * sizeof(wchar_t);
    HGLOBAL buffer = GlobalAlloc(GMEM_MOVEABLE, bytes);
    if (!buffer) {
        CloseClipboard();
        return false;
    }

    wchar_t* target = static_cast<wchar_t*>(GlobalLock(buffer));
    if (!target) {
        GlobalFree(buffer);
        CloseClipboard();
        return false;
    }

    std::wmemcpy(target, reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t*>(text.utf16()),
                 static_cast<size_t>(text.length()) + 1);
    GlobalUnlock(buffer);

    if (!SetClipboardData(CF_UNICODETEXT, buffer)) {
        GlobalFree(buffer);
        CloseClipboard();
        return false;
    }

    // 提交成功后内存所有权归系统，不能再 GlobalFree。
    CloseClipboard();
    return true;
}

QString Win32Utils::captureSelectedText(int waitMs) {
    const QString oldText = getClipboardText();
    const DWORD before = GetClipboardSequenceNumber();

    sendKey('C', true);

    QElapsedTimer timer;
    timer.start();
    while (timer.elapsed() < waitMs && GetClipboardSequenceNumber() == before) {
        Sleep(10);
    }

    QString selected;
    if (GetClipboardSequenceNumber() != before) {
        selected = getClipboardText();
    }

    // 只有复制确实发生过，或者原本就有文本时，才恢复剪贴板。
    // 这样不会在“复制失败且原本是图片”时把剪贴板清空。
    if (GetClipboardSequenceNumber() != before || !oldText.isEmpty()) {
        setClipboardText(oldText);
    }

    return selected;
}

QString Win32Utils::getForegroundProcessName() {
    const HWND hwnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    if (!hwnd) return QStringLiteral("global");

    DWORD pid = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &pid);
    if (pid == 0) return QStringLiteral("global");

    HANDLE process = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, pid);
    if (!process) return QStringLiteral("global");

    WCHAR path[MAX_PATH] = {};
    DWORD size = MAX_PATH;
    QString name = QStringLiteral("global");
    if (QueryFullProcessImageNameW(process, 0, path, &size)) {
        name = QFileInfo(QString::fromWCharArray(path)).fileName();
    }
    CloseHandle(process);
    return name;
}

void Win32Utils::forceForegroundWindow(HWND hwnd) {
    if (!hwnd || !IsWindow(hwnd)) return;
    if (GetForegroundWindow() == hwnd) return;

    const DWORD foregroundThread = GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), nullptr);
    const DWORD targetThread = GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, nullptr);

    bool attached = false;
    if (foregroundThread && targetThread && foregroundThread != targetThread) {
        attached = AttachThreadInput(foregroundThread, targetThread, TRUE) != FALSE;
    }

    SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
    BringWindowToTop(hwnd);

    if (attached) {
        AttachThreadInput(foregroundThread, targetThread, FALSE);
    }
}

QPoint Win32Utils::physicalToLogical(const POINT& pt) {
    HMONITOR monitor = MonitorFromPoint(pt, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST);
    if (monitor) {
        MONITORINFOEXW info{};
        info.cbSize = sizeof(info);
        if (GetMonitorInfoW(monitor, &info)) {
            const QString deviceName = QString::fromWCharArray(info.szDevice);
            for (QScreen* screen : QGuiApplication::screens()) {
                if (QString::compare(screen->name(), deviceName, Qt::CaseInsensitive) != 0) {
                    continue;
                }

                const qreal ratio = screen->devicePixelRatio();
                const QRect logical = screen->geometry();
                if (ratio <= 0.0) break;

                return QPoint(
                    logical.left() + qRound((pt.x - info.rcMonitor.left) / ratio),
                    logical.top() + qRound((pt.y - info.rcMonitor.top) / ratio));
            }
        }
    }

    const QScreen* primary = QGuiApplication::primaryScreen();
    const qreal ratio = primary ? primary->devicePixelRatio() : 1.0;
    return QPoint(qRound(pt.x / ratio), qRound(pt.y / ratio));
}

bool Win32Utils::isKeyDown(int vk) {
    return (GetAsyncKeyState(vk) & 0x8000) != 0;
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/UTILS/Win32Utils.h  (70 行)
// =============================================================================

#pragma once

#include <QPoint>
#include <QString>
#include <QVector>

#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#include <windows.h>

// 一个按键：虚拟键码 + 是否为扩展键（方向键、小键盘回车等）。
struct KeyInput {
    WORD vk = 0;
    bool extended = false;
};

// 解析后的组合键，例如 Ctrl+Shift+Esc。
struct KeyChord {
    QVector<KeyInput> modifiers; // 修饰键（Ctrl/Shift/Alt/Win）
    QVector<KeyInput> keys;      // 主键序列（支持同时按下多个，如 A+S+D）
    KeyInput key;                // 兼容旧代码：保留第一个主键
    bool valid = false;
    QString error;               // 解析失败原因
};

class Win32Utils {
public:
    // 解析任意组合键。支持：
    //   Ctrl / Control / RCtrl、Shift / RShift、Alt / RAlt、Win / Meta / RWin
    //   A-Z、0-9、F1-F24、方向键、Home/End/PgUp/PgDn、Insert/Delete
    //   小键盘、多媒体键、浏览器键、常见符号（+ - = ; ' / \ [ ] ` , .）
    // 注意：Ctrl+Alt+Del 是 Windows 安全注意序列，SendInput 无法模拟，会被判为无效。
    static KeyChord parseKeySequence(const QString& sequence);

    // 发送组合键。返回 false 时 error 中带原因。
    static bool sendKeySequence(const QString& sequence, QString* error = nullptr);

    // 输入任意 Unicode 文本（不受键盘布局/输入法影响）。
    static bool sendText(const QString& text, QString* error = nullptr);

    // 模拟单键，可附带 Ctrl。
    static void sendKey(WORD vk, bool withCtrl);

    // 剪贴板读写（目前只处理文本格式）。
    static QString getClipboardText();
    static bool setClipboardText(const QString& text);

    // 模拟 Ctrl+C 抓取当前选中文字，并尽量恢复原剪贴板。
    // 没有选中内容时返回空字符串。
    static QString captureSelectedText(int waitMs = 250);

    // 释放本程序注入过、但仍处于按下状态的修饰键，避免 Ctrl 卡死。
    static void clearModifiers();

    // 当前前台窗口的进程名，例如 "Code.exe"；取不到时返回 "global"。
    static QString getForegroundProcessName();

    // 绕过 Windows 前台窗口限制，把焦点还给指定窗口。
    static void forceForegroundWindow(HWND hwnd);

    // 把低级鼠标钩子给出的物理屏幕坐标换算成 Qt 逻辑坐标（多屏 + DPI）。
    static QPoint physicalToLogical(const POINT& pt);

    // 查询某个虚拟键当前是否按下。
    static bool isKeyDown(int vk);
};

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/app.rc  (5 行)
// =============================================================================

// Application icon embedded into the .exe.
// Windows only accepts .ico here (a .png cannot be embedded as an exe icon).
// "main.ico" is resolved through the /I include directory that CMakeLists.txt
// adds for this file, so it does not depend on the build working directory.
1 ICON DISCARDABLE "main.ico"

// =============================================================================
// 文件: src/main.cpp  (205 行)
// =============================================================================

#include <QAction>
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QCursor>
#include <QDebug>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QGuiApplication>
#include <QIcon>
#include <QMenu>
#include <QMessageBox>
#include <QSharedMemory>
#include <QSystemTrayIcon>
#include <QTimer>

#include "ActionEngine.h"
#include "ActionPage.h"
#include "ActionRepository.h"
#include "AppConfig.h"
#include "Constants.h"
#include "HookManager.h"
#include "MainWindow.h"
#include "WheelWindow.h"
#include "Win32Utils.h" // ExitProcess

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    // 多屏 + 不同缩放时使用小数缩放，避免轮盘位置出现整数量化漂移。
    QGuiApplication::setHighDpiScaleFactorRoundingPolicy(
        Qt::HighDpiScaleFactorRoundingPolicy::PassThrough);

    QApplication app(argc, argv);

    // 应用身份统一走 Constants，不再在这里硬编码字符串
    QCoreApplication::setApplicationName(AppConstants::APP_NAME);

    // 图标只读一次：交给 QApplication 之后，设置窗口、动作页、各种对话框都会默认继承
    const QIcon appIcon(AppConstants::getMainIconPath());
    app.setWindowIcon(appIcon);

    // 关闭所有窗口时不要退出：轮盘和设置窗口都只是隐藏。
    app.setQuitOnLastWindowClosed(false);

    // 单实例限制，防止两个钩子互相冲突。
    QSharedMemory sharedMem(QStringLiteral("Huan_Key"));
    if (!sharedMem.create(1)) {
        QMessageBox::warning(nullptr, QStringLiteral("警告"),
                             QStringLiteral("程序已经在运行中，请勿重复打开！"));
        return 0;
    }

    // 数据库统一放在用户的 AppData 下（见 AppConstants::getDbPath）：装在 Program Files 时
    // 程序目录对普通用户只读，库放那里会直接建不出来。
    const QString dbPath = AppConstants::getDbPath();

    // 老版本把库放在 exe 旁边。这里做一次性搬运：新位置还没有库、而程序目录有库时，
    // 连主库带 WAL/SHM 一起复制过去。
    // 只复制、不删除原文件 —— 万一迁移中途出岔子，旧库还在原地，数据不会凭空消失。
    const QString oldDbPath = QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath() + QStringLiteral("/data.db");
    if (!QFile::exists(dbPath) && QFile::exists(oldDbPath)) {
        if (!QFile::copy(oldDbPath, dbPath)) {
            QMessageBox::critical(nullptr, QStringLiteral("数据迁移失败"),
                QStringLiteral("检测到旧版数据库：\n%1\n\n但没能复制到新位置：\n%2\n\n"
                               "请确认旧版程序已经退出后重新运行；"
                               "或手动把 data.db 复制到上面那个目录。")
                    .arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(oldDbPath), QDir::toNativeSeparators(dbPath)));
            return -1;
        }
        // WAL 里可能还有没 checkpoint 的写入，一并带过去；带不过去最多丢最后一次写入
        const QString oldWal = oldDbPath + QStringLiteral("-wal");
        if (QFile::exists(oldWal)) QFile::copy(oldWal, dbPath + QStringLiteral("-wal"));
        const QString oldShm = oldDbPath + QStringLiteral("-shm");
        if (QFile::exists(oldShm)) QFile::copy(oldShm, dbPath + QStringLiteral("-shm"));
        qWarning() << "数据库已从" << oldDbPath << "迁移到" << dbPath;
    }

    if (!ActionRepository::init(dbPath)) {
        QMessageBox::critical(nullptr, QStringLiteral("数据库错误"),
                              QStringLiteral("无法初始化数据库：\n%1").arg(dbPath));
        return -1;
    }

    AppConfig::instance().reload();
    ActionEngine::instance();

    WheelWindow wheel;

    QSystemTrayIcon trayIcon;
    trayIcon.setIcon(appIcon); // appIcon 在 main 开头已创建，这里复用
    trayIcon.setToolTip(QStringLiteral("%1 运行中 (v%2)")
                            .arg(AppConstants::APP_NAME, AppConstants::APP_VERSION));

    QMenu trayMenu;
    trayMenu.setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral("QMenu { background-color: white; color: black; }"));

    QAction* settingsAction = trayMenu.addAction(QStringLiteral("打开设置"));
    trayMenu.addSeparator();
    QAction* quitAction = trayMenu.addAction(QStringLiteral("退出程序"));

    MainWindow mainWindow;
    ActionPage actionPage;
    wheel.setEditorWindow(reinterpret_cast<HWND>(mainWindow.winId()));

    // 开发/调试用：--settings 启动时直接打开配置窗口。
    if (app.arguments().contains(QStringLiteral("--settings"))) {
        mainWindow.show();
        mainWindow.raise();
        mainWindow.activateWindow();
    }

    if (app.arguments().contains(QStringLiteral("--preview-wheel"))) {
        QTimer::singleShot(600, &wheel, &WheelWindow::showPreview);
    }

    QObject::connect(settingsAction, &QAction::triggered, [&] {
        mainWindow.show();
        mainWindow.raise();
        mainWindow.activateWindow();
    });

    QObject::connect(quitAction, &QAction::triggered, [&] {
        sharedMem.detach();
        QApplication::quit();
    });

    QObject::connect(&mainWindow, &MainWindow::configSaved, [&] {
        wheel.reloadConfig();         // 这里会读取最新的 UI 配置并重新 resize
        wheel.update();               // 强制触发重绘，让颜色变化立刻显示
        wheel.refreshFromDatabase();
        actionPage.applyTheme(); // 新增：让动作页应用新皮肤
        actionPage.refresh();    // 新增：重新加载内容
    });

    // 动作页内新建页（或其它改动）后，通知主窗口刷新绑定预览与页数
    QObject::connect(&actionPage, &ActionPage::configSaved, [&] {
        mainWindow.refreshAll();
    });

        QObject::connect(&wheel, &WheelWindow::triggerActionPage, [&] {
        // 如果动作页当前已显示且处于展开状态，则按快捷键直接隐藏
        if (actionPage.isVisible() && !actionPage.isCollapsed()) {
            actionPage.hide();
        } else {
            QString scene = Win32Utils::getForegroundProcessName().toLower();
            actionPage.showAtCursor(scene);
        }
    });

    // 新增：呼出设置界面
    QObject::connect(&wheel, &WheelWindow::triggerSettingsWindow, [&] {
        mainWindow.show();
        mainWindow.raise();
        mainWindow.activateWindow();
    });

    // 新增：强制退出（先 detach 单实例锁，再直接结束进程）
    QObject::connect(&wheel, &WheelWindow::triggerForceQuit, [&] {
        qDebug().noquote() << QStringLiteral("触发强制退出热键，立即结束进程");
        sharedMem.detach(); // 释放单实例锁，避免下次启动提示"程序已经在运行中"
        ::ExitProcess(0);   // 硬退出：主线程或钩子死锁时也能立刻生效
    });

    QObject::connect(ActionEngine::instance(), &ActionEngine::actionFinished, [&](bool ok,
                                                                                 const QString& message) {
        if (!ok && trayIcon.isVisible()) {
            trayIcon.showMessage(QStringLiteral("动作执行失败"), message,
                                 QSystemTrayIcon::Warning, 4000);
        }
    });

    trayIcon.setContextMenu(&trayMenu);
    trayIcon.show();

    // 左键点击托盘图标时也弹出菜单（popup 非阻塞）。
    QObject::connect(&trayIcon, &QSystemTrayIcon::activated, [&](QSystemTrayIcon::ActivationReason reason) {
        if (reason == QSystemTrayIcon::Trigger) {
            trayMenu.popup(QCursor::pos());
        }
    });

    HookManager hookManager;

    const bool mouseHookInstalled = hookManager.install(
        [&wheel](WPARAM wParam, MSLLHOOKSTRUCT* mouse) {
            return wheel.handleGlobalMouse(wParam, mouse);
        });

    const bool keyboardHookInstalled = hookManager.installKeyboard(
        [&wheel](WPARAM wParam, KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT* keyboard) {
            return wheel.handleGlobalKey(wParam, keyboard);
        });

    if (!mouseHookInstalled || !keyboardHookInstalled) {
        const QString message = QStringLiteral("全局钩子安装失败（鼠标:%1 键盘:%2）。"
                                               "某些安全软件可能会拦截低级钩子。")
                                    .arg(mouseHookInstalled ? QStringLiteral("成功") : QStringLiteral("失败"))
                                    .arg(keyboardHookInstalled ? QStringLiteral("成功") : QStringLiteral("失败"));
        qWarning().noquote() << message;
        trayIcon.showMessage(QStringLiteral("启动警告"), message, QSystemTrayIcon::Warning, 6000);
    }

    const int ret = app.exec();

    sharedMem.detach();
    return ret;
}

// 文件: tests/test_database.cpp  (153 行)
// =============================================================================

// 数据库层测试：设置持久化、鼠标绑定、场景回退。
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QSqlDatabase>
#include <QSqlError>
#include <QSqlQuery>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QVector>

#include "ActionRepository.h"
#include "AppConfig.h"

namespace {

int g_failures = 0;

QTextStream& out() {
    static QTextStream stream(stdout);
    return stream;
}

void expect(bool condition, const QString& name, const QString& detail = QString()) {
    if (condition) {
        out() << "[PASS] " << name << "\n";
    } else {
        ++g_failures;
        out() << "[FAIL] " << name;
        if (!detail.isEmpty()) out() << "  (" << detail << ")";
        out() << "\n";
    }
}

} // namespace

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    QCoreApplication app(argc, argv);

    QTemporaryDir tempDir;
    if (!tempDir.isValid()) {
        out() << "[FAIL] cannot create temp dir\n";
        return 1;
    }

    const QString dbPath = tempDir.filePath(QStringLiteral("test.db"));
    expect(ActionRepository::init(dbPath), QStringLiteral("database initializes"));

    const QMap<QString, QString> defaults = ActionRepository::settings();
    expect(defaults.contains(QStringLiteral("trigger.keyboard")) &&
               defaults.value(QStringLiteral("trigger.keyboard")).isEmpty() &&
               !defaults.value(QStringLiteral("trigger.keyboard")).isNull(),
           QStringLiteral("empty keyboard setting is stored as empty string, not NULL"));

    expect(ActionRepository::getActionBySlot(0, QStringLiteral("global")).name ==
               QStringLiteral("计算器"),
           QStringLiteral("default global actions are inserted"));

    const QVector<LibraryAction> library = ActionRepository::getActionLibrary();
    expect(library.size() >= 8, QStringLiteral("legacy defaults are migrated into the library"),
           QStringLiteral("size=%1").arg(library.size()));
    expect(ActionRepository::getWheelBindings(QStringLiteral("global")).size() == 8,
           QStringLiteral("global bindings are migrated"));

    QMap<QString, QString> values = defaults;
    values.insert(QStringLiteral("trigger.keyboard"), QString());
    values.insert(QStringLiteral("trigger.mouse"), QStringLiteral("middle"));
    values.insert(QStringLiteral("trigger.threshold"), QStringLiteral("9"));
    values.insert(QStringLiteral("action.script_timeout"), QStringLiteral("12000"));

    expect(ActionRepository::saveSettings(values), QStringLiteral("settings save"));
    AppConfig::instance().reload();
    expect(AppConfig::instance().keyboardTrigger().isEmpty(),
           QStringLiteral("null QString hotkey stays empty"));
    expect(AppConfig::instance().mouseTrigger() == QStringLiteral("middle"),
           QStringLiteral("mouse trigger round-trips"));
    expect(AppConfig::instance().dragThreshold() == 9,
           QStringLiteral("integer setting round-trips"));
    expect(AppConfig::instance().scriptTimeoutMs() == 12000,
           QStringLiteral("script timeout round-trips"));

    MouseBinding binding;
    binding.button = QStringLiteral("middle");
    binding.name = QStringLiteral("粘贴");
    binding.type = 0;
    binding.payload = QStringLiteral("Ctrl+V");

    QVector<MouseBinding> bindings;
    bindings.append(binding);
    expect(ActionRepository::saveMouseBindings(bindings), QStringLiteral("mouse binding save"));

    const QVector<MouseBinding> loadedBindings = ActionRepository::getMouseBindings();
    expect(loadedBindings.size() == 1 &&
               loadedBindings.first().button == QStringLiteral("middle") &&
               loadedBindings.first().payload == QStringLiteral("Ctrl+V"),
           QStringLiteral("mouse binding round-trips"));

    // 场景动作优先，缺失槽位回退 global。
    LibraryAction sceneAction;
    sceneAction.name = QStringLiteral("场景动作");
    sceneAction.type = 0;
    sceneAction.payload = QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Shift+P");

    const int sceneActionId = ActionRepository::saveLibraryAction(sceneAction);
    expect(sceneActionId > 0, QStringLiteral("library action is created"),
           QStringLiteral("id=%1").arg(sceneActionId));
    expect(ActionRepository::bindActionToSlot(0, QStringLiteral("Code.exe"), sceneActionId),
           QStringLiteral("library action is bound to a scene slot"));

    const Action boundAction = ActionRepository::getActionBySlot(0, QStringLiteral("Code.exe"));
    expect(boundAction.name == QStringLiteral("场景动作") &&
               boundAction.payload == QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Shift+P"),
           QStringLiteral("scene binding takes precedence"), boundAction.name);

    const Action fallback = ActionRepository::getActionBySlot(1, QStringLiteral("Code.exe"));
    expect(fallback.name == QStringLiteral("记事本"),
           QStringLiteral("missing slot falls back to global"), fallback.name);

    const QVector<Action> merged = ActionRepository::getAllActions(QStringLiteral("Code.exe"));
    expect(merged.size() == 8, QStringLiteral("merged view has 8 slots"),
           QStringLiteral("size=%1").arg(merged.size()));

    const int copied = ActionRepository::copyGlobalBindingsToScene(QStringLiteral("Notepad.exe"));
    expect(copied == 8, QStringLiteral("global bindings are physically copied to a scene"),
           QStringLiteral("count=%1").arg(copied));
    expect(ActionRepository::getWheelBindings(QStringLiteral("Notepad.exe")).size() == 8,
           QStringLiteral("copied scene has its own bindings"));

    expect(ActionRepository::unbindSlot(0, QStringLiteral("Code.exe")),
           QStringLiteral("unbind scene slot"));
    expect(ActionRepository::getActionBySlot(0, QStringLiteral("Code.exe")).name ==
               QStringLiteral("计算器"),
           QStringLiteral("unbound slot falls back to global"));

    expect(ActionRepository::bindActionToSlot(0, QStringLiteral("Code.exe"), sceneActionId),
           QStringLiteral("rebind before delete"));
    expect(ActionRepository::deleteLibraryAction(sceneActionId),
           QStringLiteral("delete library action"));
    expect(ActionRepository::getWheelBindings(QStringLiteral("Code.exe")).isEmpty(),
           QStringLiteral("deleting an action removes its bindings"));

    // 关闭连接，否则 Windows 上临时目录无法删除。
    QSqlDatabase::database().close();
    QSqlDatabase::removeDatabase(QSqlDatabase::defaultConnection);

    out() << "\n";
    if (g_failures == 0) {
        out() << "ALL TESTS PASSED\n";
    } else {
        out() << g_failures << " TEST(S) FAILED\n";
    }
    out().flush();

    return g_failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: tests/test_input_sender.cpp  (171 行)
// =============================================================================

// 输入注入集成测试：真实调用 SendInput，验证组合键、扩展键和 Unicode 输入。
// 测试窗口必须在前台，否则自动跳过，避免把按键打到别的程序里。
#include <QApplication>
#include <QElapsedTimer>
#include <QTextEdit>
#include <QTextCursor>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QThread>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QWidget>

#include "Win32Utils.h"

namespace {

int g_failures = 0;

QTextStream& out() {
    static QTextStream stream(stdout);
    return stream;
}

void expect(bool condition, const QString& name, const QString& detail = QString()) {
    if (condition) {
        out() << "[PASS] " << name << "\n";
    } else {
        ++g_failures;
        out() << "[FAIL] " << name;
        if (!detail.isEmpty()) out() << "  (" << detail << ")";
        out() << "\n";
    }
}

void pump(int milliseconds) {
    QElapsedTimer timer;
    timer.start();
    while (timer.elapsed() < milliseconds) {
        QCoreApplication::processEvents(QEventLoop::AllEvents, 20);
        QThread::msleep(5);
    }
}

} // namespace

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    app.setQuitOnLastWindowClosed(false);

    QWidget window;
    window.setWindowTitle(QStringLiteral("Input Sender Test"));
    window.resize(640, 320);
    window.move(120, 120);

    auto* layout = new QVBoxLayout(&window);
    auto* edit = new QTextEdit(&window);
    layout->addWidget(edit);

    window.show();
    window.raise();
    window.activateWindow();
    edit->setFocus();

    const HWND hwnd = reinterpret_cast<HWND>(window.winId());
    Win32Utils::forceForegroundWindow(hwnd);

    // 点击窗口中心，确保它真正拿到前台键盘焦点（Windows 前台锁）。
    RECT rect = {};
    if (GetWindowRect(hwnd, &rect)) {
        SetCursorPos((rect.left + rect.right) / 2, (rect.top + rect.bottom) / 2);
        pump(100);
        mouse_event(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN, 0, 0, 0, 0);
        mouse_event(MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    }
    pump(400);
    SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
    pump(200);

    if (GetForegroundWindow() != hwnd) {
        out() << "[SKIP] test window is not foreground; input test not run\n";
        out().flush();
        return 0;
    }

    auto reset = [edit](const QString& text) {
        edit->setPlainText(text);
        QTextCursor cursor = edit->textCursor();
        cursor.movePosition(QTextCursor::End);
        edit->setTextCursor(cursor);
        edit->setFocus();
    };

    auto cursorPosition = [edit] {
        return edit->textCursor().position();
    };

    out() << "=== Single keys and navigation ===\n";

    reset(QStringLiteral("hello world"));
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Home"));
    pump(200);
    expect(cursorPosition() == 0, QStringLiteral("Home moves caret to start"),
           QStringLiteral("position=%1").arg(cursorPosition()));

    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Shift+End"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->textCursor().selectedText() == QStringLiteral("hello world"),
           QStringLiteral("Shift+End selects the line"),
           edit->textCursor().selectedText());

    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+A"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->textCursor().selectedText() == QStringLiteral("hello world"),
           QStringLiteral("Ctrl+A selects all"),
           edit->textCursor().selectedText());

    reset(QStringLiteral("abc"));
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Backspace"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->toPlainText() == QStringLiteral("ab"),
           QStringLiteral("Backspace deletes one character"), edit->toPlainText());

    reset(QString());
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("A"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->toPlainText() == QStringLiteral("a"),
           QStringLiteral("plain letter is typed"), edit->toPlainText());

    reset(QString());
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Shift+A"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->toPlainText() == QStringLiteral("A"),
           QStringLiteral("Shift produces an uppercase letter"), edit->toPlainText());

    reset(QString());
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Shift+1"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->toPlainText() == QStringLiteral("!"),
           QStringLiteral("Shift+1 produces '!'"), edit->toPlainText());

    reset(QString());
    Win32Utils::sendText(QStringLiteral("中文😀"));
    pump(400);
    expect(edit->toPlainText() == QStringLiteral("中文😀"),
           QStringLiteral("Unicode text input (including emoji)"), edit->toPlainText());

    reset(QStringLiteral("line1"));
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("NumpadEnter"));
    pump(200);
    expect(edit->toPlainText() == QStringLiteral("line1\n"),
           QStringLiteral("NumpadEnter inserts a newline"), edit->toPlainText());

    reset(QStringLiteral("one two three"));
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Left"));
    pump(200);
    Win32Utils::sendKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Left"));
    pump(200);
    const int position = cursorPosition();
    expect(position > 0 && position < 13, QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Left moves by word"),
           QStringLiteral("position=%1").arg(position));

    out() << "\n";
    if (g_failures == 0) {
        out() << "ALL TESTS PASSED\n";
    } else {
        out() << g_failures << " TEST(S) FAILED\n";
    }
    out().flush();

    return g_failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: tests/test_key_parser.cpp  (148 行)
// =============================================================================

// 按键解析 / 变量替换的离线单元测试。
// 不发送真实按键，不会影响当前桌面。
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDate>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QTime>

#include "VariableResolver.h"
#include "Win32Utils.h"

namespace {

int g_failures = 0;

QTextStream& out() {
    static QTextStream stream(stdout);
    return stream;
}

bool hasModifier(const QVector<KeyInput>& modifiers, WORD vk) {
    for (const KeyInput& modifier : modifiers) {
        if (modifier.vk == vk) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

void expect(bool condition, const QString& name, const QString& detail = QString()) {
    if (condition) {
        out() << "[PASS] " << name << "\n";
    } else {
        ++g_failures;
        out() << "[FAIL] " << name;
        if (!detail.isEmpty()) out() << "  (" << detail << ")";
        out() << "\n";
    }
}

void expectChord(const QString& input, WORD vk, bool extended, int modifierCount) {
    const KeyChord chord = Win32Utils::parseKeySequence(input);
    const QString detail = QStringLiteral("valid=%1 vk=%2 ext=%3 mods=%4 err=%5")
                               .arg(chord.valid)
                               .arg(chord.key.vk)
                               .arg(chord.key.extended)
                               .arg(chord.modifiers.size())
                               .arg(chord.error);
    expect(chord.valid && chord.key.vk == vk && chord.key.extended == extended &&
               chord.modifiers.size() == modifierCount,
           QStringLiteral("parse \"%1\"").arg(input), detail);
}

void expectInvalid(const QString& input) {
    const KeyChord chord = Win32Utils::parseKeySequence(input);
    expect(!chord.valid, QStringLiteral("reject \"%1\"").arg(input),
           QStringLiteral("unexpected success: vk=%1").arg(chord.key.vk));
}

} // namespace

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    QCoreApplication app(argc, argv);

    out() << "=== Key parser ===\n";

    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Shift+A"), 'A', false, 2);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+V"), 'V', false, 1);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("F12"), VK_F12, false, 0);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+F4"), VK_F4, false, 1);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Win+Shift+S"), 'S', false, 2);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Meta+Space"), VK_SPACE, false, 1);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Left"), VK_LEFT, true, 1);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Shift+Esc"), VK_ESCAPE, false, 2);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("NumpadEnter"), VK_RETURN, true, 0);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Num5"), VK_NUMPAD5, false, 0);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("MediaPlayPause"), VK_MEDIA_PLAY_PAUSE, true, 0);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Shift+Alt+Win+K"), 'K', false, 4);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl++"), VK_OEM_PLUS, false, 2);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Plus"), VK_OEM_PLUS, false, 2);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Print"), VK_SNAPSHOT, true, 0);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Menu"), VK_APPS, true, 0);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral("Alt+F4"), VK_F4, false, 1);
    expectChord(QStringLiteral(";"), VK_OEM_1, false, 0);

    expectInvalid(QString());
    expectInvalid(QStringLiteral("Ctrl"));
    expectInvalid(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Unknown"));
    expectInvalid(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+A+B"));
    expectInvalid(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+Alt+Del"));

    {
        const KeyChord chord = Win32Utils::parseKeySequence(QStringLiteral("Ctrl+?"));
        expect(chord.valid && hasModifier(chord.modifiers, VK_CONTROL) &&
                   hasModifier(chord.modifiers, VK_SHIFT),
               QStringLiteral("Ctrl+? auto-adds Shift"),
               QStringLiteral("valid=%1 mods=%2").arg(chord.valid).arg(chord.modifiers.size()));
    }
    {
        const KeyChord chord = Win32Utils::parseKeySequence(QStringLiteral("RAlt+Tab"));
        expect(chord.valid && chord.key.vk == VK_TAB && hasModifier(chord.modifiers, VK_RMENU),
               QStringLiteral("RAlt is an extended modifier"),
               QStringLiteral("valid=%1").arg(chord.valid));
    }

    out() << "\n=== Variable resolver ===\n";

    VariableContext context;
    context.scene = QStringLiteral("Code.exe");
    context.selection = QStringLiteral("abc");
    context.clipboard = QStringLiteral("XY");
    context.now = QDateTime(QDate(2026, 1, 2), QTime(3, 4, 5));

    const QString expanded = VariableResolver::expand(
        QStringLiteral("{scene}|{selected}|{clipboard}|{date}|{time}|{datetime}"), context);
    expect(expanded == QStringLiteral("Code.exe|abc|XY|2026-01-02|03:04:05|2026-01-02 03:04:05"),
           QStringLiteral("core variables"), expanded);

    expect(VariableResolver::expand(QStringLiteral("hello {unknown}"), context) ==
               QStringLiteral("hello {unknown}"),
           QStringLiteral("unknown variable is preserved"));

    expect(VariableResolver::expand(QStringLiteral("{not a var}"), context) ==
               QStringLiteral("{not a var}"),
           QStringLiteral("text with spaces is not a variable"));

    expect(VariableResolver::expand(QStringLiteral("100%"), context) == QStringLiteral("100%"),
           QStringLiteral("lone percent is preserved"));

    expect(VariableResolver::expand(QStringLiteral("{newline}"), context) == QStringLiteral("\n"),
           QStringLiteral("newline variable"));

    const QString env = VariableResolver::expand(QStringLiteral("{env:WINDIR}"), context);
    expect(!env.isEmpty() && !env.startsWith(QLatin1String("{env:")),
           QStringLiteral("{env:WINDIR}"), env);

    const QString envPercent = VariableResolver::expand(QStringLiteral("%WINDIR%"), context);
    expect(!envPercent.isEmpty() && !envPercent.startsWith(QLatin1Char('%')),
           QStringLiteral("%WINDIR%"), envPercent);

    out() << "\n";
    if (g_failures == 0) {
        out() << "ALL TESTS PASSED\n";
    } else {
        out() << g_failures << " TEST(S) FAILED\n";
    }
    out().flush();

    return g_failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

// =============================================================================
// 文件: tests/test_script_runner.cpp  (222 行)
// =============================================================================

// 脚本执行器集成测试：真实启动 Python / cmd / PowerShell，但不会注入鼠标键盘。
// 用 ASCII 输出，避免控制台代码页干扰。
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QElapsedTimer>
#include <QEventLoop>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QStandardPaths>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QTimer>

#include "ScriptRunner.h"

namespace {

int g_failures = 0;

QTextStream& out() {
    static QTextStream stream(stdout);
    return stream;
}

void expect(bool condition, const QString& name, const QString& detail = QString()) {
    if (condition) {
        out() << "[PASS] " << name << "\n";
    } else {
        ++g_failures;
        out() << "[FAIL] " << name;
        if (!detail.isEmpty()) out() << "  (" << detail << ")";
        out() << "\n";
    }
}

bool writeFile(const QString& path, const QString& content) {
    QFile file(path);
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate)) return false;
    return file.write(content.toUtf8()) >= 0;
}

ScriptRunner::Result runSync(const QString& command, const QString& input, int timeoutMs,
                             qint64* elapsedMs = nullptr) {
    ScriptRunner::Result result;
    bool done = false;

    auto* runner = new ScriptRunner();
    QEventLoop loop;
    QTimer safety;
    safety.setSingleShot(true);
    QObject::connect(&safety, &QTimer::timeout, &loop, &QEventLoop::quit);

    QElapsedTimer timer;
    timer.start();

    runner->run(command, input, timeoutMs, [&](const ScriptRunner::Result& value) {
        result = value;
        done = true;
        loop.quit();
    });

    if (!done) {
        safety.start(timeoutMs + 6000);
        loop.exec();
    }

    if (elapsedMs) *elapsedMs = timer.elapsed();
    if (!done) {
        result.ok = false;
        result.error = QStringLiteral("测试框架等待超时");
    }

    delete runner;
    return result;
}

} // namespace

int main(int argc, char* argv[]) {
    QCoreApplication app(argc, argv);

    out() << "=== Script command resolution ===\n";

    {
        const ScriptCommand spec =
            ScriptRunner::resolve(QStringLiteral("python -u \"C:\\a b\\x.py\""));
        expect(spec.valid() &&
                   QFileInfo(spec.program).fileName().startsWith(QLatin1String("python"),
                                                                 Qt::CaseInsensitive) &&
                   spec.arguments.contains(QStringLiteral("C:\\a b\\x.py")),
               QStringLiteral("explicit python command line"), spec.error);
    }
    {
        const ScriptCommand spec = ScriptRunner::resolve(QStringLiteral("\"C:\\a b\\x.py\""));
        expect(spec.valid() &&
                   QFileInfo(spec.program).fileName().startsWith(QLatin1String("python"),
                                                                 Qt::CaseInsensitive) &&
                   spec.arguments.size() == 1 &&
                   spec.arguments.first() == QStringLiteral("C:\\a b\\x.py"),
               QStringLiteral(".py path is mapped to python"), spec.error);
    }
    {
        const ScriptCommand spec = ScriptRunner::resolve(QStringLiteral("\"C:\\a b\\x.ps1\""));
        expect(spec.valid() && spec.arguments.contains(QStringLiteral("-File")),
               QStringLiteral(".ps1 path is mapped to powershell -File"), spec.error);
    }
    {
        const ScriptCommand spec = ScriptRunner::resolve(QStringLiteral("\"C:\\a b\\x.bat\""));
        expect(spec.valid() &&
                   QFileInfo(spec.program).fileName().compare(QLatin1String("cmd.exe"),
                                                              Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0 &&
                   spec.arguments.contains(QStringLiteral("/c")),
               QStringLiteral(".bat path is mapped to cmd /c"), spec.error);
    }
    {
        const ScriptCommand spec = ScriptRunner::resolve(QStringLiteral("notepad.exe"));
        expect(spec.valid() && spec.arguments.isEmpty(),
               QStringLiteral("plain executable is passed through"), spec.error);
    }
    {
        const ScriptCommand spec = ScriptRunner::resolve(QString());
        expect(!spec.valid(), QStringLiteral("empty command is rejected"));
    }

    out() << "\n=== Script execution ===\n";

    QTemporaryDir tempDir;
    if (!tempDir.isValid()) {
        out() << "[FAIL] cannot create temp dir\n";
        return 1;
    }

    const QString echoScript = tempDir.filePath(QStringLiteral("echo.py"));
    writeFile(echoScript,
              QStringLiteral("import sys\n"
                             "sys.stdin.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')\n"
                             "sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')\n"
                             "data = sys.stdin.read()\n"
                             "print('收到:' + data)\n"));

    {
        const ScriptRunner::Result result =
            runSync(QStringLiteral("python \"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(echoScript)),
                    QStringLiteral("中文ABC"), 8000);
        expect(result.ok, QStringLiteral("python exits 0"), result.error);
        expect(result.stdoutText.contains(QStringLiteral("收到:中文ABC")),
               QStringLiteral("python stdin/stdout is UTF-8"),
               result.stdoutText.trimmed());
    }

    {
        const ScriptRunner::Result result =
            runSync(QStringLiteral("python \"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(echoScript)),
                    QString(), 8000);
        expect(result.ok && result.stdoutText.trimmed() == QStringLiteral("收到:"),
               QStringLiteral("empty stdin becomes EOF"), result.stdoutText.trimmed());
    }

    const QString failScript = tempDir.filePath(QStringLiteral("fail.py"));
    writeFile(failScript,
              QStringLiteral("import sys\n"
                             "sys.stderr.write('boom')\n"
                             "sys.exit(3)\n"));
    {
        const ScriptRunner::Result result =
            runSync(QStringLiteral("python \"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(failScript)),
                    QString(), 8000);
        expect(!result.ok && result.exitCode == 3,
               QStringLiteral("non-zero exit code is reported"),
               QStringLiteral("code=%1 error=%2").arg(result.exitCode).arg(result.error));
        expect(result.stderrText.contains(QStringLiteral("boom")),
               QStringLiteral("stderr is captured"), result.stderrText.trimmed());
    }

    const QString sleepScript = tempDir.filePath(QStringLiteral("sleep.py"));
    writeFile(sleepScript, QStringLiteral("import time\nwhile True:\n    time.sleep(0.2)\n"));
    {
        qint64 elapsed = 0;
        const ScriptRunner::Result result =
            runSync(QStringLiteral("python \"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(sleepScript)),
                    QString(), 600, &elapsed);
        expect(!result.ok && result.timedOut,
               QStringLiteral("timeout kills infinite script"), result.error);
        expect(elapsed < 8000,
               QStringLiteral("timeout returns promptly"),
               QStringLiteral("%1 ms").arg(elapsed));
    }

    const QString batchScript = tempDir.filePath(QStringLiteral("hello.bat"));
    writeFile(batchScript, QStringLiteral("@echo off\r\necho bat-ok\r\n"));
    {
        const ScriptRunner::Result result =
            runSync(QStringLiteral("\"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(batchScript)),
                    QString(), 8000);
        expect(result.ok && result.stdoutText.contains(QStringLiteral("bat-ok")),
               QStringLiteral("batch file runs via cmd /c"),
               result.error + QStringLiteral(" | ") + result.stdoutText.trimmed());
    }

    if (!QStandardPaths::findExecutable(QStringLiteral("powershell")).isEmpty()) {
        const QString psScript = tempDir.filePath(QStringLiteral("hello.ps1"));
        writeFile(psScript, QStringLiteral("Write-Output \"ps-ok\"\n"));
        const ScriptRunner::Result result =
            runSync(QStringLiteral("\"%1\"").arg(QDir::toNativeSeparators(psScript)),
                    QString(), 20000);
        expect(result.ok && result.stdoutText.contains(QStringLiteral("ps-ok")),
               QStringLiteral("PowerShell script runs"),
               result.error + QStringLiteral(" | ") + result.stdoutText.trimmed());
    } else {
        out() << "[SKIP] PowerShell not installed\n";
    }

    out() << "\n";
    if (g_failures == 0) {
        out() << "ALL TESTS PASSED\n";
    } else {
        out() << g_failures << " TEST(S) FAILED\n";
    }
    out().flush();

    return g_failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}
